FOREWORD
We feel a sense of deep satisfaction in presenting to
the scholars of Indian Philosophy this neatly edited version
of Ajita which is a commentary by Paritosa Misra of
Mithila on the Tantravārttika of Kumarila, the
illustrous of the Mimāmsã philosophers.
most
At least nine commentaries are known to have been
written on the Tantravarttika from time to time by various
scholars. Some of these are no more in existence whereas
many of them are languishing in the Manuscripts libraries.
The only commentary published so for is the Nyayasudha
of Bhatta Someśvara which is, however, so extensive and
elaborate that it suits the temperament and fulfills the
raquirements of only an advanced scholar and not of a
a learner.
The commentary which strikes a middle path and
suits the purposes of a learner and a scholar alike is
decidedly the one edited by us here. It is lucid yet not
shallow, scholarly yet not terse. It reveals the meaning
of the original with utmost clarity without adding any-
thing redundant and without dropping anything signi-
ficant. It is coherent and concise.
Nothing is known about its author Paritosa except
that he lived on the banks of Ajita, a river of unknown
provenance (indentified with Gandaki by the editors).
The internal evidences seem to indicate that he probably
flourished in the 13th century.
A number of scholars have cherished the desire to
edit and publish this rare gem of commentaries. The
CC-0. Ganga Nath Jha Campus. Central Sanskrit Uni. Digitized by Sri Muthulakshmi Research Academy
[page 8]
( vi )
first and the foremost among these was Sir Ganganatha
Jha himself who procured a transcript of Ajita from the
Govt. Oriental MSS Library of Madras in the later part of
his life (i.c. in the late thirties of the current century). A
Mimamsa scholar of the calibre of Sir Ganganatha Jha
would certainly have been the most competent person to
accomplish this task. However, Destiny did not favour
him with this glory. He died in 1941 without even
undertaking the work in his hand. The Ganganath Jha
Research Institute established in his memory in 1943
inherited his personal library and the transcript of Ajita
hibernated till about 1976 when it caught the attention
of Dr. Murlidhar Pandey, a senior Reader in our
Vidyapeetha, who expressed his desire to critically edit it.
Not much progress was made though, since Dr. Pandey
was posted in Delhi shortly thereafter with an assignment
of different nature following which he proceeded to
Jammu to take charge of the Kendriya Sanskrit Vidya-
peetha as its Principal. A number of other requests for
supply of a copy of the transcript were received in this
Vidyapeetha from scholars desirous to undertake the job
but it was thought proper that this Vidyapeetha-as the
academic heir of Sir Ganganatha Jha-itself should
come forward to fulfil this last wish of Sir Jha.
Post of a Lecturer in Mimāmsa was consequently
created in this Vidyapeetha on which Dr. Kamala
Nayan Sharma was appointed; Dr. Kishor Nath Jha,
Reader in Sarvadarśana was deputed to participate in
Mīmamsā seminars and refresher courses and Prof.
Gajanana Shastri Musalgaokar, an eminent Mīmāmsaka
was appointed as Guest Professor under the Shastra
Chudamani scheme of the Rashtriya Sanskrit Sansthan.
This energetic group assisted by Km.Archana Chaturvedi,
our Research Assistant in Vedanta, undertook the job of
editing this valuable commentary and carried out the task
CC-0. Ganga Nath Jha Campus. Central Sanskrit Uni. Digitized by Sri Muthulakshmi Research Academy
[page 9]
(vii)
within a comparatively short span of three years. Dr.
Kishora Nath Jha accompanied by Dr. K.E. Govindan had
to undertake journies to distant Madras and Trivendrum as
well in order to collate their readings with the manuscripts
deposited there since their transcripts were difficult to be
obtained. Thus there is no denying to the fact that no
amount of mental or physical labour was spared to bring
this rare work to light and make it scientifically perfect.
My sincere thanks are due to all the above mentioned
scholars who have really done their job with utmost
dedication.
It is also my pleasant duty to acknowledge grate-
fully the help and assistance received by us from Prof.
Kurio Harikai who has published four fascicules of the
chapter I of Ajitā in Act a Eruditiorum No. 1-4 (1983-86)
and has kindly sent us copies of them. They have been
very useful to us in reconstructing the text.
Whether the work has come up to the expectations
of the scholars or not, only they are competent to decide.
We crave their gracious indulgence for any mistakes
committed by us due to our ignorance or incompetence
and humbly hope that the publication of this since long
overdue commentary on Tantravārttika shall be welcomed
by the scholars.
G. N. Jha Kendriya Sanskrit Vidyapeeth
Allahabad
5th March, 1988
G. C. Tripathi
Principal
CC-0. Ganga Nath Jha Campus. Central Sanskrit Uni. Digitized by Sri Muthulakshmi Research Academy

kumārilabhaṭṭaviracitaṃ tantravārttikaṃ paritoṣamiśrapraṇītayā ajitāṭīkayā
saha prakāśyate iti jñātvā nitarāmānandamanubhavāmi | paritoṣa miśrasya ṭīkā
nedānīṃ yāvat prakāśitā kutrāpi | ato viduṣāṃ viśeṣataśca mīmāṃsādhyā-
yina|bhapūrvo'yam avasarastantravārttikatantrāvabodhane | vidupādhyāpakena ḍā0 kiśora
nāthena tatsahayogibhyāṃ ḍaॉ0 kamalanayana śarmaṇā kumārī arcanā caturvedī
śubhodayayā ca mahatā yatnena pañcamātṛkābhyaḥ pāṭhasamīkṣaṇapūrvakaṃ sampādito'yaṃ
grantha iti sarvathā sādhuvādamarhati sampādakaḥ | gaṃgānāthajhā-kendrīya-saṃskṛtavidyā-
pīṭhaprācāryo'pi dhanyavādārhaḥ granthasyāsya prakāśanāya |
mudritapustakasya patrāṇi paṭhikhā jñātametat yad ajitāṭīkādhṛtaḥ
pāṭhastantravāttikasya sandehāspadasthaleṣu saṃśayanirasanāya prabhavediti | na kevala-
midaṃ bahuṣu sthaleṣu mūlagranthasya arthāvabodhanaṃ sukhakaraṃ syāt |
ślokavāttiṃka-tantravāttiṃka-ṭupaṭṭī kānāmadheyānāṃ tridhā vibhaktānāṃ
kumārilakṛtaśābarabhāpyavyākhyānānāṃ ślokavāttike eva dārśanikānāmabhiniveśo
mahān vividhadarśanānāṃ samīkṣaṇāt | paraṃ tantravārttike'pi mahatī śāstracarcā vartate
yuktipramāṇamanusṛtya | ataḥ ajitopetaṃ tantravārttikaṃ sarvathā abhivandanīyam |
kṣīṇatāṃ gatā mīmāṃsāparamparā sabalā bhavedanayā ityāśāste |

bubhutsāyāṃ kariṣyamāṇāneka vicāraprasādhyo'rthasiddhavadapadiśyate - codanā
pramāṇamiti | anapekṣalakṣaṇatvācca prāmāṇyasyāsmaryamāṇakartṛ katvena prasiddhā-
pauruṣeyatvasya vedasya codaneti parigṛhīta evārthaḥ autpattikasūtre' sphuṭī
bhaviṣyati | tatra ca padapadārthasaṃbandhasaṃghātanityatvāt vedavākyānāṃ prāmāṇyo-
papattayaḥ, na codanātvasya | tasmāt vedavākyāni pramāṇānīti vivakṣitam |
tāni ca codanā eveti | teṣāṃ tatsaṅkīrttanaṃ dharmapramitiśaktipradarśanārtham |
tena śābdaprāmāṇyapratijñānamevedaṃ vaidikīṣu codanāsu vyavasthitam | ata evāha
bhāṣyakāraḥ - codanā hi bhūtaṃ bhavantaṃ bhaviṣyantamityādi [ 1|1|4 | ] anyathā
tvasiddhe sāmānyaprāmāṇye viśeṣānavasarādanenaiva sāmānyaviśeṣayavivakṣā
syāt | tena svatantrasya śabdarāśervedākhyasya vākyāni codanātvād dharme
pramāṇānīti sūtrārthaḥ |
evaṃ sati vedaikadeśatvāt sakalena prāmāṇyapratipādanaprabandhenārthavādādī-
nāmapi prāmāṇyaṃ pratijñāya prasādhitamapi prāmāṇyaśakticodanātvānabhi-
vyakteravasīdatīti | teṣāṃ codanāntargatisamarthopayogapratipādanamārabhyate |
tadidamuktam - siddhapramāṇabhāvasyeti | vedasya hi dharme prāmāpyaṃ pratijñātam |
tasya hi sarvaprakāraṃ padapadārthasambandhasaṃghātanityatayā vākyārthasya mūlavattayā
visamvādābhāvenāsaṃśayitārthatvena ca prāmāṇyamavagamitam | evaṃ codanātvaṃ
pratītisiddhamaṅgīkṛtya sarvasya siddhapramāṇabhāvasya vedāvayavānāmāśaṅkitānu-
payogānāmupayogo'bhidhīyata iti |
astu vā codanātvenopalakṣitasya śabdaviśeṣasya dharme prāmāṇyena vidhā-
nam, evamapi sakalavedasya parigrahaḥ | tadadhyayanahetukatvena dharmajijñāsopanyā-
sāt | na hi ekadeśapramāṇikāyāṃ sakalādhyayanaṃ hetuḥ syāt | apauruṣeyatvā-
dinā ca sakalaprāmāṇyapratipādanāt | loke sanniyamāditi [ mī0 sū0 1|1|
26|] ca vaidharmyāpanyāsena apauruṣeyasya kṛtsnasya vedasya kriyārthena samāmnāya
iti pravṛttiprayojanābhidhānāt | śakyante ca parigrahītuṃ vākyaṃ hi codanā |
asti ca loke kārakapadānāmiva stāvakapadānāmapyekavāvayatā | krathyeyaṃ

[ prathamādhyāyasya
vedāvayavatvāviśeṣādupasthāpitānāṃ mantrārthavādanāmadheyānāṃ tulyanyāyatayā
prayojanāntarābhāvācca dharma eva prasaktaṃ prāmāṇyamavabodhaśaktyabhāvāda-
sambhavat prayojanavattvena pramāṇalakṣaṇaupayikatvena ca prasaṅgāt vicāryate |
eṣa ca ślokārthaḥ - siddhapramāṇabhāvasya nityatayā vedasya codanāstāvat
dharmapramāṇatvena siddhāḥ | samprati itareṣāmapi tadavayavānāṃ dharma evopayoga
ucyate sarvaśa iti | sarvaprakāraṃ yāvantaḥ prakārāḥ dharmapramityupayoginaḥ
sambhavanti dharmapramāṇena vidhinā yad yadapekṣitaṃ tattat pratipādanadvāreṇetyarthaḥ |
adhuneti cāvasaraprāpti vadan prāsaṅgikatāṃ darśayatīti vyākhyeyam | prasaktiśca
darśitā vedasya vidhyarthavādamantrāṇāmiti | vidhiriti tredhā vibhaktasya
vedasyaiko bhāgo vidhyuddeśo'bhidhīyate | tatrāpyupayogapratipādanasya āśa-
ṅkitānupayoga nāmadheyasya ca tathābhāvāt nāmadheyamabhipretya vidhi-
śabdaḥ | evaśva vyācakṣate -
-
yathaudumbarādhikaraṇe vidhimantareṇāpi sādhyasādhanasambandhamupadarśayatā
vidhyanupayogaḥ pūrvapakṣe, siddhānte ca vidhimantareṇa tasyāsiddheḥ | tasyaiva
tatra prāmāṇyamiti vidhyupayogo darśayiṣyata iti | evamapi nāmadheyasya
parigraho nānyatra pradeśād vaktavya iti |

payogitvamuktam | tatra mantrārthavādayoḥ sākṣāt vidhāyakāpekṣitasmṛti-

kriyārthena samāmnāya' [ mī0 sū0 1|1|25 | ] ityupasaṃhārād vidhipratiṣedhayoreva
prāmāṇyaṃ pratipāditam | na ca tadvyatiriktaśabdagamyatvaṃ dharmādharmayoḥ |
nāpyanadhigatārthabodhanaṃ muktvānyaḥ śabdasya vyāpāro'stītyuktameva | ataśca
yāvantyeva sādhyasādhanetikartavyatāvācitvena vidhipratiṣedhāntargatāni teṣāṃ
bhavatu prāmāṇyaṃ yāni tvatiriktārthavādamantranāmadheyānupātīni so'rodīdipe

prathamādhyāyasya
dharmādharmayorapramāṇamatadarthatvāt | yathāśrutagṛhītānāṃ tāvatpratītyanupalabdheḥ
prasiddhamevātadarthatvam | atha kayācicchandavṛttyā tādarthya kalpyeta, evamapi
vyavasthāhetvabhāvānna dharmādharmayoravadhāraṇaṃ syāt | yadeva hi vākyaṃ gṛhītaṃ
tadevādhyāhāravipariṇāmādibhiryatheṣṭaṃ kalpayituṃ śakyate | tadyathā 'so'rodī-
dityevameva' tāvadvākyaṃ viśiṣṭapuruṣācaritopanyāsadvārā rodanakartavyatāparam |
atha vā mahatāmapyevaṃvidhāḥ pramādāḥ saṃbhavanti | tasmātprayatnena varjayi-
tavyamiti | ato vidhipratiṣedhayorasphuṭatvāddharmādharmatvena nirṇaye śaktyabhāvaḥ |
śāstradṛṣṭavirodhādayaśca sthitā eva |
yattu bhūtānvākhyānamātraṃ yathāvasthitaiḥ pratipādyate | tatra yadyapi satyatva-
dharmātirikte'rthaśabdasya prāmāṇyaṃ nāstītyuktameva | yau tu pramāṇāntaragocarau
dharmādharmau tayorapyavidhitvānna sambhavati | ato'prāmāṇyameveti | teṣāṃ
bhavatu prāmāṇyamiti | teṣāṃ bhavatu prāmāṇyamiti ca sāmānyānujñānamevedam |
dharmādharmayorapramāṇamatadarthatvāditi | idānīṃ sādhyamānasyārthasya nigamanam
tataśca sarvathaivāprāmāṇyamabhimatamiti | kathamatadarthatvamiti cet atrāha
yathāśruteti |
atha kayācicchabdavṛttyeti bhāṣyokta evādhyāhārādyupanyāsaḥ svādhyā-
yādhyayanavidhibodhitasākalyopapādakaḥ kriyate | yadeva hi vākyamiti |
adhyāhārādīnāmekatamasya niścayo'śakyaḥ kimutādhyāhriyamāṇarūpaviśeṣa-
nirūpaṇamityabhiprāyaḥ |
tadyatheti | siddhe'pyadhyāhāre viśeṣe nirūpaṇāśaktimudāharati
śāstradṛṣṭavirodhādaya iti | karttavyatvopadeśe yāvanto virodhāstānupanyasyati
dṛṣṭavirodhaśva rodanavapotkhananādīnāmaśakyatvam |
yattu bhūtānvākhyānamātramiti | anvākhyānaṃ pratipādyate ityodanapārka
pacatītivanneyam | kriyata iti vaktavye tāmeva kriyāṃ pratyayena vivakṣan
tadanugrahārthamanuvādabhūtaṃ dhātuṃ prayuṅkta e | athavā yathāvasthita bhūtānvākhyāna-
mātram | yattu kenacid vādinā pratipādyata iti yojyam |
yadyapi satyatvamiti | satyatvabhāṣayā na prāmāṇyamabhyupagacchati |
viphalatvena tātparyāsambhavāt svārthe'pyaprāmāṇyamiti |

[ prathamādhyāyasya
kalpanā śakyā | na hi loṣṭaṃ paśyatastaddarśanaṃ niṣprayojanamiti suvarṇadarśanatā
kalpyate | sarvāṇyeva ca pramāṇānyupayujyamānamanupayujyamānaṃ vārthamātmagocaratā-
pannaṃ gamayanti | tenaiva caiṣāṃ hānopādānopekṣābuddhayaḥ phalatvena varṇyante | anyathā
hi upādānamevaikaṃ phalaṃ syāt |
|
api ca pramāṇotpattyuttarakālaṃ prayojanavattvamaprayojanavattvaṃ vā vijñāyate
ma tu tadvaśena pramāṇodbhūtiḥ | tasmādyo yadrūpo'rthaḥ pratīyate sa tathaiva prayojana-
vattvamaprayojanavattvaṃ vā pratipadyate | na hi prayojanavadeva pramātavyamiti kaścinniyama-
heturasti | yatrāpi tāvatsvādhīnapramāṇaviniyogastatrāpyetad durlabhaṃ kimutā-
buddhipūrvanityāvasthitavedotthāpitajñānagrāhyaṃ | na ca vedasya prayojanavadarthā-
pramāṇaviṣayaṃ hi prayojanārthī prayojanatayā gṛhṇāti na tu tasya viṣaya-
mupakalpayatīti | na hi loṣṭaṃ paśyata iti puruṣapravṛttyupanyāsena prayojanā-
thitā darśitā | yadi tu pramāṇānāmevaiṣa svarasaḥ puruṣāpekṣitapratipādanaṃ
nāma syāt | tato vidhyekavākyatāmagatvā na paryavasānaṃ syāt | na tu
tadastītyāha sarvāṇyeva pramāṇānīti | anyathā hya pādānamevaika iti |
-
nanu heyārthapramitirapi hānāṅgatayāpekṣitā | satyam, heyaṃ tu
nāpekṣitam, anupayogāt | upayogitayā ca prameyatā na siddhyatīti
vaktumupakrāntam | athavā hānaṃ duḥkhopalambhabhayādanapekṣitasya duḥkhasyā-
styupalambhe kiṃ kasyaciddhāneneti | aśakyañcedaṃ prayojanavattayā viṣaya
nirūpaṇamityāha api ceti | jñātvā prayojanavattāṃ prayojanavatī pramāṇaṃ
netavyam | anupajāte ca pramāṇe anupalabdhasya na prayojanavattvajñānam |
ato'napekṣitaprayojanasyaiva pramāṇasyodaya iti |
-
nanvetadapyasti puruṣādhīnaḥ pramāṇavi [ ni ] yogaḥ | kimiti cāsā-
vanupayogini tanniyogakleśamanubhavet | tenāprayojanatvamapi viruddhamata
āha nahi prayojanavadeva pramātavyamiti | kaściditi | kimiti nāstī-
tyāha yatrāpīti | na hi yadabhipretya niyujyate tadbodhayati kintu yatra
śaktamevaṃpuruṣādhīnapravṛttāvapi pramāṇe yattvādityavat svatantraṃ prakāśakaṃ tatra
dūranirastam | prayojanavata eva pramāṇena viṣayīkaraṇamityāha - kimutā
buddhipūrvanityāvastheta [ vasthiteti ] |

bhidhānaśakti: prathamamavadhṛtā | ayameva hi parīkṣākālo varttate kīdṛśaṃ puna-

na ca

kimarthamayaṃ niṣprayojanamabhidadhātītyasya paryanuyogasyābhāve kāraṇamāha-

tasmāt yathaivāgnihotrādivākyānāṃ gamyamānaprayojanavattvādānarthakyaṃ niṣpramāṇaka-

brāhmaṇa ityupapannaṃ vacanamiti | tataśca etadapi śāstravirodhasyo-
dāharaṇaṃ yuktaṃ, na dṛṣṭavirodhasyeti | yathā vṛkṣatvaṃ prāgabhidhānavyutpatteriti |
yathā abhidhānavyutpattau satyāṃ vyavasyantī vivicya vṛkṣatvaṃ naivaṃ pūrvamityarthaḥ |
parastu pratipattyabhāvamudāhṛtaṃ matvā dūṣayati naitattulyamiti |
api ca vyutpannaśabdo'pīti | bhavati śabdasyaivendriyānugrāhakatvam na punara-
nyasyetyabhipretyocyata iti |
nanu mā bhūt pratyakṣagamyā jātiḥ | evamapyupanayanamadhyayanaṃ brāhmaṇa-
śabdapravṛttau nimittamiti | tayoḥ pratyakṣatve pratyakṣaviruddhatā jñānavacanasye-
tyāśaṅkayāha - na copanīteti | upanītamadhyayanaṃ copanītasya vādhyayana-
miti |
dhūma evāgnedivā [ ? ] | nanu adhyāpanayājanapratigrahā brāhmaṇaśabda-
prayogaṃ na vyabhicaranti, kṣatriyādiṣu niṣedhādityāśaṅkayāha-adhyāpanādyapīti |
1. na copavītā iti mudritapāṭhaḥ |

mānarthakyābhidhānenākriyārthānāṃ hyetat pratijñātam | ucyate, siddhānte
vākyaśeṣatvāsambhavādaprāmāṇyamiti pratijñātam | tatra yathā vākyaśeṣatvā-
sambhavaḥ tatsāmānyenārthavādākṣepādihāpyuktam | vakṣyate ca prarocanābuddhistu
navotpadyata iti | idānīṃ tu tathāpi kathamaprāmāṇyaṃ svātabhyeṇa kimiti
prāmāṇyaṃ na sambhavatītyetannirākarotīti etadevāsya svayamākulatvamiti |
cayanamāśrayati ca pṛthivīniṣedhārthaṃ tanniṣedhena niṣedhati ca yathāśrutārtha-

[ prathamādhyāyasyā

prāk
svargasādhanatvamadhyayanasya pratipattavyam uta kiñcidavāntara kāryapratīti-

syānuṣṭhānāṅgatvaṃ niyamāt sāmarthyena bodhitam, padārthapadākṣarāvadhāraṇā-
dhyayanānāṃ na niyamena karmāvabodhāṅgatvaṃ jñātam | ato'dhyayanamavāntara-

nanu yadi phalavatprakaraṇe paṭhyante teṣāṃ pāṭha eva prakaraṇinā gṛhyata

yathā pūrvī dhāvatītyukte yadyapyaparo gamyate tathāpi na kāryeṇa

anyathānupapattyā ceyaṃ tebhyaḥ kalpyate | sarvapramāṇa pratyastamaye cānyathānupapatti-
bhavati | pramāṇābhāvaśca yaḥ sarvaprakāraṃ prayatamānasya sa pramāṇam | tadyadi
kenacidupadeśenātideśena vā na lapsyāmahe tato darvihomanyāyena vidhyuddeśasyaiva dve
śaktī kalpayiṣyāmaḥ | atha tu kenacid dūrasthenāpi setsyati tatastadanusārastā-
vatkartavya ityevaṃ sākāṅkṣo vidhirāste | tathā vāyurve kṣepiṣṭhā devatetyevamādyapi -
svādhyāyādhyayanavidhinā puruṣārthatayopanītaṃ yacchavayate tatkuryādityupavandhācca
sahasaiva tenāsaṃbadhyamānamapuruṣārthatvena ca pūrvavadakṣarādiṣvaparyavasyad bhūtānvā-
khyānavākyārthaṃ yāvad bhūtam | tatrāpi tu sākāṅkṣameveti yastena lakṣyamāṇa:
sādhanānurūpasādhyotpattidvāreṇa kṣipradevatāsāvyaṃ karma kṣiprameva phalaṃ dāsyatīti
prāśastyarūpo'rthaḥ so'nantarapravṛttavighyudde śākāṅkṣitatvātpuruṣārthe dvāratāṃ śaknoti
pratipattumiti parigṛhyate | so'yaṃ naṣṭāśvadagdharathavatsaṃprayogaḥ | evaṃ ca na
prarocanānyakṛtā karmāṅgam | na cārthavādapadaḥ prayojanāntaraṃ sādhanīyamityā-
yathā
vyāpṛtānīti na taistatkriyata iti - na kāryeṇa yujyata iti | na vākyārthaṃ
pūrayatītyarthaḥ | yataḥ śabdenaiva śabdasya pūraṇam | na cānyaparādavagatasya
śābdatvamiti - atideśena veti | vācanikasyātideśasyāśaṅkā |
kvacidvacanaṃ bhavati | asya pūrvo bandhuriti | asya vidheḥ pūrvasya
vidheryo bandhurarthavādaḥ sa eveti | davihomanyāyeneti | yathā davihomānā-
mupadeśātideśābhyāmalābhāditikartavyatāyāḥ sādhanatvamanupapādyamānaṃ sādhana-
pratītidāḍharyāt | evamaṅgīkṛtamitikarttavyatayā vināpi upakāra eṣāṃ
svamahimapratiṣṭhita iti | tathātra śabdāntaropaneyamapi prāśastyaṃ
vidhyuddeśa evārthāt bodhayatīti kalpayiṣyāma iti | svādhyāyādhyayana-
vidhineti | yacchakyate tatkuryādityupabandhāt tena sannihitena vidhinā
sahasaivāsambandhaḥ svādhyāyavidhivaśenākṣarādiṣvaparyavasānamiti | yadi
vidhyuddeśasya sambhavato dve śaktī vyarthaṃ tarhi arthavādādhyayanam | ata
evañca [na] prarocaneti | niyamaḥ prayojanamityarthaḥ | na cārthavāda-

pādāya vidhistutyanvayapradarśanamidaṃ na bhavati | ata āha— sa~va na
bhūtinimittasyeti | bhūtinimittasyālambhasyeyaṃ stutyā yojanā | yata
ālambhāt bhūtirbhavati, ato'sau praśasta iti na vidheḥ | yā vidhyu-
ddeśenārthavādānāṃ pratyayaparityāgena yojanopapāditā saivedānī bhāṣya-
kāreṇocyata iti vadatā tatroktopapattiḥ, sarvaivaṃ vyākhyāyāṃ darśiteti |
kimiti bhūtikāmenālabdhavyamityayamapi vivakṣitaḥ sambandha ityatra
bhāṣyakāreṇoktam | bhinnāvimāvarthāviti | bhūyastvādarthavādānāṃ na stuti-
stutyasambandhavivakṣāyāṃ siddhāyāṃ yadyaparo'pi sambandho vivakṣyeta tatra
vākyaṃ bhidyeteti | atremāvityabhidhānasya viṣayaṃ darśayati

kathyate | sa iti prakṛtāpekṣaḥ kutaḥ tatpratyayāt | taddhi prakṛtaṃ pratīyate |
atha vā tacchabdasya prakṛtagrāhitvaṃ prasiddham | sa ityukte tacchandapratyayāt
sa evārthaḥ| evaṃ tasya yadaśru tadrajatamiti sambandhaḥ | sarvā ceyamuparitanasya
nindāgranthasyopapattiriti tadanantaraṃ tadabhidhānamupapadyate | kosī granthastaṃ
darśayati | yo barhiṣi rajataṃ dadyāt purāsya samvatsarād gṛhe rodanaṃ bhavediti |
kena hetunā tadevaṃ bhavatīti tadupapādyate kāraṇānurūpatvātkāryasya | rodana-
prabhavarajatadānādrodanotpattistasmānna deyamiti, sakalamadānasyopapattiriti |
nindayā taccheṣatvamarthavat | guṇavādastu śabdālambanaṃ rudraśabdotthāpita vijñānavaśena
rodanasāmānyato'dṛṣṭakalpanā | aśruṇaśca śauklyādyadi nāmaitatkaṭhinaṃ bhavettato
rajatasadṛśaṃ bhavedityutprekṣya tatprabhavanindā | dhanatyāgenātyantodārasyāpi
gṛhajana: pīḍayata iti tatsāmānyādvā rodanopanyāsaḥ | evaṃ yena kenacidā-
rudraśabdotthāpiteti | rudraśabdotthāpitaṃ jñāpanaṃ prakṛtipratyayārthanirīkṣaṇāt
rodanakartteti | tadanena kathaṃ bhagavān viṣayokriyate | yadi nārodīt tasmāda-
rodīditi | sāmānyato dṛṣṭarūpā rodanaviṣayiṇī kalpanā so'rodīditi |
nanu guṇavādamālambya śabdo vṛtta ityuktam | na cānena gauṇatā
samarthyate | ucyate, kalpanāśabdena tacchabdaprayogo'bhidhīyate | pravṛtti-
nimittaṃ guṇa itaraiḥ padaiḥ kathyate | yo'rodīt sa rudraśabdena jñāyate |
bhagavati ca rudraśabdo dṛśyate | taṃ dṛṣṭvā tenaiva guṇasāmānyenārodī-
dityucyate | yathā hi siṃhaguṇayogād devadattaḥ siṃha iti | yadi
nāmaitat kaṭhinamiti | anenāpi guṇayoga evocyate | tadyataḥ prathamaṃ
bhavati tat tena sadṛśam | atra cāśrutasādṛśyamasti | tena prakṛti-
vikāragatasādṛśyarūpaguṇayogāt prakṛtivikāraśabdaḥ yadi nāmeti | dravya-
tvameva vailakṣaṇyam | itarathā sādṛśyamityuktamiti | yathā so'rodīvi-
tyādīnāṃ prarocanaupayikaṃ gauṇamālambanaṃ labdhaṃ naivaṃ prajāpativapotkhananā-
divākyānāṃ labhyate, ato'rthaśūnyatvam | asati cārthe lakṣaṇānupapatteḥ
prarocanāpi kathamiti paricodyaḥ kathaṃ punaranutkhinnāyāṃ vapāyāmityādinā |

[ 53
lamvanena nindāvijñānotpattiḥ pratiṣedhopakāriṇīti mukhyāthībhāve'pyadopaḥ |
evaṃ vapotkhananādivākyayojanā | svātmavapotkartanenāpi hi viśiṣṭaprayojanārthaṃ
karmaṇi kriyante kimuta bāhyadhanatyāgeneti stutiḥ | yathā netramapyuddhṛtyāyaṃ
dadātīti loke'pi tyāginaṃ stuvantīti | vṛttāntaparyavasāyī ca vedastatra
prāmāṇyamapratipadyamānaḥ stutau satyatvānnānyatrānveṣaṇamarhatīti niṣprayojanopākhyā-
nasatyatayā nārthaḥ | śabdabhāvanāṅga 'cārthavādāḥ | sā ca pravṛttirvijñānamātre
ṇaivopayujyate nārthe | arthātmikāyāṃ tu sarvatrāvisaṃvādaḥ siddha eva | atho
cyeta, asadanvākhyānena stutinindātvamiti sutarāṃ tatra pratīyate | kāmaṃ paramārthe
vaktāro bhavanti kātra stutinindā vā satyamevaitaditi | asatye tu yasmādanyatra

bhāṣyakāreṇa parihṛtam asadvṛttāntānvākhyānaṃ stutyarthenetyādinā | tade

jñānamevopayujyata iti darśitam | ekadeśadarśanācchabda vijñānāditi ca
tenārthavādairjñānameva vidhāyakaśabdānugrahāyopādīyate nārtha iti |

pramāṇa-
nanvevamasatyapi padārthe vākyārthaḥ siddhyet | siddhayati yadi

tantravārtikam
[ prathamādhyāyasya
atha vobhayoḥ kevalamiśraliṅgayormadhye paṭhito'yamarthavādaḥ | tatra ubhaya-
devatyayoranyā praśaṃsā, itarayoḥ punariyaṃ yasmāddivāgnirādityaṃ gacchati
tasmātsūrya eva tadā jyoti: | evaṃ naktamagniratastau vyavasthitāveva yaṣṭavyā-
viti stutiḥ | kathaṃ tu dhūma evetyavadhāraṇā | dūrasthairbhūmnā divā dhūma eva gṛhyate
rātrāvaciriti kenacidaṃśena stutyālambanam ||12||
pravare prakriyamāṇe yajamāno vaded devāḥ pitara ityādi | tatpraśaṃsārtha-
muktamabrāhmaṇo'pi brāhmaṇo bhavati pravarānumantraṇeneti | tatra prasiddha brāhmaṇa-
tvānāmeva brāhmaṇyalābho niṣprayojana iti tadupapattyarthamuktam
-
na caitadvidma
iti | jñāyamāne tvajñānava vanaṃ durjñānatvāt | yatsukhenājñātaṃ tadajñānameva | tacca
stryaparādhanimittam | satyapi ca stryaparādhe yadi mātureva kṣetriṇo vā putraḥ
syāttatastayoḥ prasiddhajātitvānnaiva durjñānatā bhavet | tayorapyevamevaṃ tatpūrvajayo-
rityanādinyāyena jātiravadhāryetaiva | yatastu 'mātā bhartrā pituḥ putra'
atha vobhayoriti | bhāgyodāhṛtayoreva mantrayoḥ śeṣo'yaṃ bhavatviti
vaktavye | nanu sannidhāvayaṃ paṭhitaḥ, tasyaivāyaṃ śeṣaḥ | kathamaniyamena
vyākhyāyata iti | codyānavakāśāyoktam - ubhayormadhye paṭhita iti |
dūrasthairbhūmnā iti | yathā siṃhaśabdaḥ siṃhamatikramya gauṇe prayoge
tadīyād guṇānuvādād te dṛśirapi darśanaviṣaye'tiśayadūratve tathāvidha-
ścaivakāreṇa dhūme'vadhāryate, nāciriti cārciṣi niṣidhyate |
yadyevaṃ kena darśanamupāttam | na hyatra devadattapadavat padāntaramasti |
ucyate, siṃhapadādapi prathamasiṃhagatā eva guṇāḥ pratīyante | yadā
devadattapadasāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ tadā tadyogaḥ | iha anyasyānupādānād darśanāti-
śayenaiva darśanopādānam | yathā siṃhagatatvena tadguṇānāṃ vacanaṃ niṣphala-
miti | itarākāṅkṣenaivamatra vaiphalyam | arciṣi tadabhāvasya pratipādya-
mānatvāt | tena dhūmo yathā dṛśyate tathā nācirityasyārthe dhūmo
dṛśyate nācirityuktamiti || 12 ||
yat sukhenājāta [na] miti | tatrāpi jānātinā tadviṣayaṃ saukaryamupā-
dāya tasya na jñānaniṣedhaḥ | mātā bhartrā iti | dhāraṇāt poṣaṇācca
smartṝṇāṃ darśanamiti | 'mātā bhartrā pituḥ putro yena jātaḥ sa
iti smaraṇāt ||13||
eva saḥ'
1. nājñānamiti mudritapustakapāṭhaḥ | 2. bhastrā iti mudritapustakapāṭhaḥ |

ūrgudumbara ityādi tāvadurjo'varudvyā ityasyopapattitayaikavākyabhūtaṃ na
vicchinnaṃ stāvakam | na hyannamudumbara ityukte puruṣaṃ prati praśastatā gamyate |
ūrjā'varudvyā iti tu prarocayati | tadupapattiritareti saṃgacchate | tasmāt kṛtsnena
phalaṃ prarocanā vā | tatra yadyapi tāvalliṅādiḥ syāttathāpi sutarāmarthavādatayā
gṛhṇīyāt | kimuta vartamānāpadeśo yo vidhisiddhyarthameva | prakaraṇācca yāgo -
pakāre prayojane labhyamāne kimityavācakaṃ phalaṃ kalpayiṣyāmaḥ | prakaraṇaṃ vākyena
bādhyata iti cenna | avirodhāt | yadi hyakāṃśaggrāhitvena vākyaprakaraṇe
saṃnipatetāṃ tathā sati bādhaḥ syāt | iha tu prakaraṇamaudumbaratāvidhi
gṛhṇāti so'pyūrgudumbara ityādilabhyāṃ prarocanām | tatra tadviśiṣṭaudumbaratva-
grāhiṇaḥ prakaraṇasya ko virodhaḥ | tasmāt phalavidhisāmarthyābhāvāt prakaraṇā-
vāvena niveśasambhavo nātikramitavyaḥ | prarocanāpi ca vākyaśeṣādupapadyamānā
nāthapattyā vivyuddeśādeva kalpitā bhaviṣyati | anyatra vidhirapi hi tāvattāṃ
kalpayet | iha tu sa svayameva duḥsthitaḥ kiṃ prarocanayā kariṣyatīti | na
ca stutyopayuktasya punaḥ phale'pi pratiṣṭhādāviva vyāpāro yujyate | rātri-
satrāṇāṃ hyananyaparatvād gurvyapi kalpanāśrīyate | atra tu prakaraṇādasti
prayojanasiddhirityuktam | jātiviśeṣasya ca yogyatayaiva kāṣṭāṅgatvaṃ prasiddhaṃ
na phalārthatā | yūpaśabdo'pi ca karmāviṣṭakāṣṭhavacana ityupanītamātrameva gṛhṇāti |
ataśca prasiddhe pārārthye'rthavādatā | yattu yūpe nirākāṅkṣo jāti pratīti
śaravabādho bhaviṣyati | na cābādhaprakāro'sti | kāmyapakṣe'pi tadabhyupa-
gamāt | etāvāṃstu viśeṣaḥ | tava puruṣārthatvādbhinnaviṣayeṇa satā kathañcit-
-
tatrāha - urgadumbara ityādi | tāvaditi | tatra yadyapi liṅādibhiḥ
syāditi | dvitīyaṃ pakṣaṃ sarvavidhisarūpāṇāmarthavādatvaṃ vyutpādayati-
sutarāmarthavādatayeti | arthavādasvarūpatvādityabhiprāyaḥ | na cārthavāda-
sarūpo'pi phalāpekṣayā phalavidhirvaktavya ityāha prakaraṇācceti |
avācakamiti | avidyamānavācakaṃ phalavācakāśrayaṃ hi phalavidhisāmarthyaṃ
tadasati vācake kalpanīyaṃ tatkimiti - kalpayiṣyāma iti | na ca stutyopayukta-
syeti | caturthādhikaraṇasya prasaṅgādupanyāsaḥ jātiviśeṣasyeti |

śabdenābhihite iti |

nanu nāyamidānīṃ mantravicārāvasare pūrvapakṣa āśrīyate | tatkatha-

evaṃ manyata iti | so'rodīdityevamādīnāṃ lakṣaṇayā
prāśastyaparatvaṃ, tasya ca vidhyuddeśānupraveśaḥ kathañcidadhyayana vidhibodhitārthī-
payogitvabalena yatra kriyate tatra mantropayoge kaḥ sandehaḥ | vivakṣitavacanatve
tāvat vidhyapekṣitānuṣṭheya smṛtisāmarthyasya pratyakṣatvāt spaṣṭa evopayogaḥ |
vacanavivakṣāyāmapi prayājādivaditi 1 tasmādānarthakyaśaṅkayaivo-

cinteyaṃ syāt | na tu pūrvapakṣe dharmapramityupayogasaṃbhavaḥ | brīhyādivattu
sādhanatvena syāt | pūrvapakṣe 'siddhapramāṇabhāvasya dharme vedasya sarvaśa'
iti prāmāṇyenopayogipratipādanamārabdham | tathā cārthavādādhikaraṇe
ānarthakyapakṣa evoktam |

puruṣarāja ityarthānyatvam kauśāmbinirityanarthakatvam | yastvagnīndrāviti kvacit-
prayogaḥ sa chāndaso'gnervābhyarhitatvasya vivakṣayeti mantavyam | tulyakakṣārtha -

tu kiṃ nimittamiti na vidmaḥ | tena mantraprasiddhī vākyaniyamāt
[mī0 sū0 1|2|32 |] ityapi sūtram | atha vāstvaprakaraṇādhītavākyaniyamāt |
na hyarthābhidhāne'nyadīyasyātmīyasya ca viśeṣaḥ |
atha vā mantrapauruṣeyavākyayostulyārthābhidhānasāmarthya mantravākya-

dṛṣṭe'rthe mantrasya dhyānādervā kaścidviśeṣa ityadṛṣṭārthatā ||32||

hyavidvānvihito'stītyavaśyaṃ tāvadagnīdhātmīyāḥ padārthāḥ
prāgeva karmaprakramādavagantavyāḥ | tatra praṣaḥ piṣṭapeṣaṇanyāyenārthaṃ jñāpayituma-
śaknuvannadṛṣṭārtho bhavati | atha smārako mantra ityucyate | tadapi
nāsti | brāhmaṇajñānābhyāsapāṭhavanimitta saṃskārādeva tatsiddheḥ | saṃskārābhi-
vyaktiheturapi pūrvapadārthasamāptirbrāhmaṇameva vā bhaviṣyati nārtho mantraiḥ | tatra
yaducyate saṃskāraviśeṣo bhaviṣyatīti nāsāvadṛṣṭādanyaḥ saṃbhavatyataḥ
pūrvapakṣāpattiḥ | yadi hi pratīto'pyartho naiva dṛṣṭaṃ sādhayati tato yattaddūramapi
gatvāvaśyaṃ kalpanīyaṃ taduccāraṇadeva varamiti || 33 ||
na catuḥśṛṅgādi kiñcitkarma tatsaṃbandhi vā prakṛtau vikṛto vā vidyate |
yadyapi ca guṇavādena kiñcitsyāttathāpi tadanuṣṭhānābhāvānna tatsmṛtyā kāryam |
vācakamiti | puruṣarāja iti | puruṣasya viśeṣaṇatvādanyārthatvam |
kauśāmbiniriti | nātra kauśāmbyā apādānatvapratītau kāraṇamasti |
nirgata iti ca gatyarthasya yathānvākhyānameva prayoge tādarthyādakriyāyuktānāṃ
ca nirādīnāmavācakatvādathantirasyāpyabhāva ityānarthakyamiti | abhya-
hitatvasya vivakṣayeti | ardhyāhataṃ pūrva nipatatīti smaraṇāt ||32||
nanvadṛṣṭo'pi saṃskāraḥ pratyāyanād bhaviṣyati | kathaṃ punaḥ pūrvapakṣā-
pattirityucyate | tatrāha - yadi hi pratīto'pyarthaṃ iti ||33||
tathāpi tadanuṣṭhānābhāvāditi | tasya gauṇasvarūpasyānuṣṭhānābhāvāt |
tathā pratipādanamadṛṣṭāya yajñaprayogo'nena pratipādyaḥ 1 tasya ca

sūktamiti ca dyotitam | tatra sṛṇiraṅkuśaḥ saraṇasādhanatvāttamarhantau tatra

nanvārṣa paripālanamīdṛśaṃ bhūtāṃśena ṛṣiṇā jarāmaraṇavimoka-
kāmenāśvinau sṛṇyevetyādinā stutāviti | tadevamanityapuruṣācaritaṃ kathaṃ
nityavākyārtha nirūpaṇopāya ityāśaṅkyāha yathaiva vyākaraṇeneti |

mūlāntarasaṃbhavaḥ |
virodhe tvanyamūlatvamiti syādardhavaiśasam ||
mūlāntaraṃ nirastaṃ ca sāmānyenaiva yatpurā |
tadanuprāṇyate paścāt virodhenetyatikriyā ||
tenāsāṃ śrutimūlatvaṃ sarvadaiva vyavasthitam |
mūlāntarapraveśe vā kiṃ tat prāmāṇyatṛṣṇayā ||
kiñca bhrāntyādimūlānāṃ sambhavāsambhavāśrayaḥ |
smṛtī: prati viruddho'yaṃ vādhapakṣo na yujyate ||
tenāsāṃ yadi vā naiva kvacidasti pramāṇatā |
sarvatrāvyāhatā vā syāt na tvardhajaratīyatā ||
[ 12
avirodhe śrutirmūlamiti | yasyaiva manvādivacanasya śrutimūlatvameva,
nānyamūlatvamiti rūpaṃ nirūpitamaviruddhasmṛtiviṣaye tasyaiva viruddhasmṛtiviṣaye
bhrāntyādimūlatvamityarddhābhyāṃ vaiśasaṃ viruddhatā | tulyanyāyasya samudāyasya |
arddhana vārddhāntarasya vaiśasaṃ tulyanyāye samudāye syāditi | evaṃ sarvatra virodha-
sambhāvanāyāṃ durlabhaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ syādityuktam | idānīmasatyapi virodhe
mūlāntarasambhāvanā vārayitumaśakyetyāha mūlāntaraṃ nirastamiti |
yathocitakriyāsampattāvapyanuparamoḍatikriyā tayā ca pūrvasiddhasya : nāśo'bhi-
matāntarānavāptiśca pākādiṣu prasiddhā | seyamāpadyeta smṛtiprāmāṇyaṃ
pratipādya punastatraivāparaṃ viśeṣaṃ pratipādayata iti |
-
kiñca bhrāntyādimūlānāmiti | bādho hi svataḥ pramāṇasya balavadvi-
rodhādbhavati | tenābādhapakṣe' svataḥ prāmāṇyasiddhaye bhrāntyādīnāmasambhava
ityaṅgīkarttavyam | bādhasiddhaye ca teṣāmeva sambhava iti | bhrāntyādimūlānāṃ
yau sambhavāsambhavau tayorāśrayatvādviruddho'yaṃ smṛtīḥ prati bādhapakṣa iti | pūrvaṃ
virodhaviṣaye mūlāntaramanujñātamaśakyamanyatra nirākartum | tasmādaikyarūpya-
maṅgīkriyatāmityupasaṃhṛtam | tenāsāṃ śrutimūlatvamiti | idānīṃ tu mūlāntara-
viṣaya sambhavāsambhavau na sambhavataḥ | tasmādaikarūpyamastvityupasaṃharati
tenāsāṃ yadi veti |

na vidadhyāttatastādṛk smṛtistanmūlatāṃ tyajet |

virodhe'pyadhunā yuktamevaṃ prāpte'bhidhīyate ||

pramāṇatvaṃ pratītaṃ yadujjvalattadapahanutam |
ekastāvadayaṃ doṣaḥ syātpratītiviparyayāt ||
tathā tadapramāṇatvaṃ yadabhāvapramāṇakam |
bhāve satyatha bhāvena vinaiva parikalpyate ||
so'pi syādaparo doṣaḥ pramāṇārthaviparyayāt |
aṅgīkṛtyāpi tau doṣau pūrvaṃ kenāpi hetunā ||
prayogāntarakāle tu punardoṣadvayaṃ bhavet |
yadabhāvapramāṇatvaṃ pūrvametasya kalpitam ||
sampratyapanuvānasya doṣa eko hi jāyate |
pratyakṣaṃ dṛḍharūpaṃ ca yatpurastānnirākṛtam ||
eṣa jāyeta doṣo'nyastadujjīvayataḥ punaḥ |
evamekatra catvāro doṣā vākye pradarśitāḥ ||
eta eva prasajyeran dvitīye'pi prakalpite |
evameṣo'ṣṭadoṣo'pi yadvrīhiyavavākyayoḥ ||
vikalpa āśritastatra gatiranyā na vidyate |
brīhiśāstrapravṛttī hi yavaśāstreṇa kṛṣyate ||
śrotā tatra pravṛtto'pi brīhiśāstreṇa kṛṣyate |
dvābhyāmaśvamukhīvacca śrutibhyāmabhitaḥ samam ||
śroturākṛṣyamāṇasya balābalamapaśyataḥ |
ekasminnupasaṃhatu buddhi yuktyā na paśyataḥ ||
ubhayorapramāṇatvaṃ pratighātātprasajyate |

siddho lokapravādo'yamekānekavināśinām |
sarvanāśe samutpanne hyarvaṃ tyajati paṇḍitaḥ ||
sa cāyamubhayornāśa ubhayārthāparigrahāt |
miśrairvā yajamānasya miśrāṇāṃ vidhyadarśanāt ||
niyamārthe hya ubhe śāstre yavavrīhyavidhātṛṇī |
prāpte cānyanivṛttyaṃśaḥ phalamarthāt pratīyate ||
vrīhayo nirapekṣā hi jñāyante yāgasādhanam |
yavāścaivamatasteṣāṃ miśratvaṃ nāvakalpate ||
naiva vrīhibhiriṣṭaṃ syādyavairna ca yathāśrutaiḥ |

prathamādhyāyasya
ṛtvijo hi prayogamadhyapatitaṃ yajamānaṃ prakrāntakarmāviśyasamāpanīya -
tvanibaddhasamāptyuttarakālabhāvi svācchandyaṃ ca viditvā kāryavattāvelāyāmetra
khalagata'prādhānyavibhāgavyāvṛttabhṛtakavat svayamutpādyotpādya tāni tānyā deyakāni
śraddhājanakārthavādapuraḥsaraṃ yācante | pratyakṣaśrutivihita deyāntara nidarśanavyāmo-
hitaśca yajamānaḥ śraddadhānatayā tathaiva pratipadya tebhyaḥ prayacchatīti taireṣā smṛti:
pravartitā syādityāśaṅkāyāṃ vedamūlatvaṃ nānumīyate | pūrvavacca lobhapūrvakatva-
kalpanamevopapannamiti nirṇayātsandehanivṛttiḥ | idaṃ ca bhāṣyakāreṇa pradarśayatā
tulyakāraṇatvāt pūrvatrāpi pradarśitameveti yojayitavyam |
etāvattvahādhikaraṇadvaye'pi vaktavyam |
smṛtīnāṃ śrutimūlatve dṛḍhe pūrvaṃ nirūpite |
virodhe satyapi jñātuṃ śakyaṃ mūlāntaraṃ katham ||
smṛtīnāṃ śrutimūlatva iti | na tāvadatra vastugato virodho vidyate,
anuṣṭhānaviṣayatvāt | yo'pi hi vrīhīnupādatte nāsau yavānāṃ yāgasādhanatva-
śaktimavajānīte' | kintvekenaiva sādhyasiddhau kimapareṇeti nopādatte |

tālvādidhvanipreraṇavat pauruṣeyatvena paribhavitavyāni |

sa paṭhadbhiḥ prakāśyeta smaradbhirveti tulyabhāk ||

tātvadidhvanipreraṇavaditi | pratyakṣe'pi śrutiviṣaye puruṣādhīnatā
darśitā | sati ca puruṣādhīnatve yathā vedarūpe'nyathākaraṇe cāsti bahubhyaḥ
syāt nirākriyetyevaṃ pauruṣeyatve doṣaṃ nirasyaniścaya evaṃ mahājanapari-
gṛhītamanvādivacanadarśite parokṣe'pi vidyamānamapi hi pratyakṣaṃ varṇasamūha-
mātraparyavasitam nānādisampradāyāvagatatvaṃ tasya nirūpayituṃ śaktam |
tamevārthaṃ sphuṭīkaroti vedo hīdaśa evāyamiti | evaṃ pratyakṣānumāni-
kayorvedayoḥ puruṣapratyayādhīnatvamaviśiṣṭaṃ pratipādya samprati prāmāṇyo-
pasthāpakasmaraṇādhīnatvaṃ saṃskārādhīnatvaṃ cāviśiṣṭamiti darśayati
anuccāraṇakāle ceti | yathaiva pratyakṣopahitaḥ saṃskārastadanurūpaṃ smaraṇaṃ
janayatīti manvādismaraṇāt kāraṇaparamparāmanumāya manvādipratyakṣasya
vedasya siddhirevamadhīyamānasya pratyakṣānurūpamapi smaraṇaṃ yathāsmaraṇaṃ hi
pāṭhaḥ, smaraṇaṃ ca saṃskāradvāreṇa pratyakṣānurūpamataḥ sampradāyakāle pratyakṣī-
kṛtasya pāṭho'yamiti niścīyate | viśeṣastvetāvānanyadīyena smaraṇena
kvacidanyapratyakṣonnayanaṃ kvacidātmīyenātma pratyakṣasya | na cāsmatprāmāṇye
viśeṣaḥ | sarvameva hi kasyacidanyadīyaṃ kasyacidātmīyamiti | atha na
pratyayitapuruṣapraṇītatvāṅgīkaraṇenaiva smṛtiśāstrasya virodhyekadeśe'prāmāṇya-
mucyate | kintvekadeśe virodhidarśanāt śāstrasyaivāpratyayita puruṣapraṇītatvam |
tatrāpyāha smṛtiśāstraṃ ca yadyekamiti | upapadyata evaitat kintvevaṃ
mahājanaparigrahastasya na syāt | yadā tu vedamūlānumāne kāraṇaṃ mahājana-
parigrahaḥ sarvatra samāno dṛśyate | sarvatra cānutriyamāṇo bhūyiṣṭhaṃ mūlabhūto
vedastadā tvapraṇītatvena vedamūlatve niścita eva śāstrasya virodhyekadeśā-

arthabhedavyavasthānādavirodho'vadhāryate ||

[ 1857
na caivajātīyakā arthāḥ
padārthānāṃ vyavadhātāro bhavantīti | itarathā hyaṅgapradhānārthatvenāśrīyamāṇe
śūnyahṛdayavacanamevaitatsyāt |
tasmādācamanādīnāṃ kratubhiḥ saṃgrahe sati |
paścātprāptakrameyattākālaśāstrāvirodhitā ||
nanvidaṃ yajñopavītamudāhartavyam | darśapūrṇamāsādiṣu 'upavyayate deva-
lakṣmameva tatkurute' iti kratvaṅgapratyakṣavidhānāt | tathā kāṭhake cānārabhya-
vādavidhinā sarvayajñagatayajanayājana saṃvandho'dhyāpanādhyayana saṃbandhaśca śrūyate |
prasṛtena vai yajña ena devā svargaṃ lokamāyannaprasṛtenāmurānparābhāvayan | pramṛto
ha vai yajñopavītino yajño'prasṛto'nupavītino yadvai kiñca brāhmaṇo yajñopavī-
tyadhīte yajata eva tattasmādyajñopavītyevādhīyīta yājayedyajeta vā yajñasya
prasṛtyā iti' | tathācamanavidhirapi 'dakṣiṇata upavīyopaviśya hastāvavanijya
trirācamya dviḥ parimṛjya darbhāṇāṃ mahadupastīryopasthaṃ kṛtvā prāṅmukha upaviśya
svādhyāyamadhīyīta' iti, yadyapi brahmayajñaprakaraṇe śrūyate tathāpi 'vedoccāraṇa-
saṃbandhāttadaṅgatvenāpi' iti tāvajjñāyamānaṃ sarvayajñānāṃ mantravatprayogitvāttadaṅga
bhavati | tathā ca 'na somenocchiṣṭā bhavanti' iti prāptācamanapratiṣedhārtha-
mavadhāryate | dakṣiṇācāratvaṃ tu—

svayamajñātamūlāśca śiṣṭācārapramāṇatām |
vadanto'pi na śobhante smṛtikārāstato'dhikāḥ ||
smṛtikāravacanārtho hi śiṣṭairācaritavyaḥ śiṣṭatvāya | anyathā hi
tadanapekṣāḥ svātantryeṇa vyavaharamāṇā duṣṭā bhaveyurna śiṣṭāḥ |
na ca teṣāṃ śrutirmūlaṃ vyavahārasya dṛśyate |

api vā kāraṇāgrahaṇe prayuktāni pratīyeran [mī0 sū0 1|3|7 ] |

śiṣṭairācaryamāṇānāṃ satāṃ godohanādivat |
phalasaṃbandhamaprāptaṃ
bodhayacchāstramarthavat ||
[ 197
na hi tadevaikaṃ śāstrapramāṇakaṃ yasya svarūpamapi tata evāvagantavyam |
anekākārasya hi prameyasya kaścidevākāra: kenacitpramāṇena pramīyate | tatra
pratyakṣādyavagate'pyācārasvarūpe dadhigodohanādivatphalasaṃbandha: śāstreṇāvagamyate |
yāgādiṣvapi ca naiva svarūpajñānena śāstramapekṣitam | phalasaṃbandhamātrasyaivātīndri-
yatvena tadapekṣitasvāt | ato na nāmopalakṣaṇāntareṇa śāstraṃ pravṛttamupalabhya śiṣṭāḥ
pravṛttāḥ | sarvakālaṃ tu śiṣṭavyavahāraśāstrayoraviyogādvyavahārādevāpoddhṛtya
kenacitsvargādisādhanatvena niyamyamānāḥ kādācitkatvaparityāgena nityaprayojyā
vijñāyante | teṣāṃ cāryāvarta nivāsiśiṣṭaprayojyatvamevopalakṣaṇaṃ vedenāpi sarasvatī-
vinaśana plakṣaprasravaṇādivadupāttamiti śakyamanumātum |
nanu śāstrārthakāritvācchiṣṭatvaṃ gamyate yadā |
śiṣṭatvena ca śāstroktirityanyonyāśrayaṃ bhavet ||
naiva teṣāṃ sadācāranimittā śiṣṭatā matā |
sākṣādvihitakāritvācchiṣṭatve sati tadvacaḥ ||
pratyakṣavedavihitadharmakriyayā hi labdhaśiṣṭatvavyapadeśā yatparamparāprāpta-
manyadapi dharmabuddhayā kurvanti tadapi svargyatvāddharmarūpameva |
godohanasya phalasaṃbandhamātraṃ śāstragamyamevamācārāṇāmapīti na vaiphalyam |
ata eva netaretarāśrayatvamityāha - ato na nāmopalakṣaṇāntareṇeti |
yadyapi śiṣṭācāraḥ śiṣṭairācaraṇīya iti yadevopalakṣaṇaṃ tadeva
vidheyam, na tayorbhedastathāpi śāstravaśena pūrvaśiṣṭai rācaryamāṇo'rthastenaivo-
palakṣaṇenāpatkālān prati vidhīyate | taiśvācaryamāṇo'rthaḥ tebhyo'parakālān
pratītisarvakālaṃ śiṣṭācāraśāstrayorudbhavādupapattiḥ |
sarasvatīvinaśaneti | sarasvatyā vinaśanaṃ pṛthivyāṃ tirobhāvaḥ |
tasyā eva plakṣāt prabhavaḥ | plākṣaprasravaṇaṃ te karmaviśeṣasya deśopalakṣaṇa-
tayopātte yathā vedavihitānuṣṭhānopalakṣito vedena svavidheyānuṣṭhānopa-
lakṣaṇatvenopādīyata ityaviruddham, tayorviśeṣarūpeṇa bhedāditi | yaduktaṃ tatra

tenāhorātra paurvāparyavadanā ditvā dvedatadarthānāmitaretarāśrayatvāprasaṅgaḥ

matikramyopacikīrṣā, atha vā caṇḍālamapi saśarīraṃ svargaṃ nayāmīti sva-

tparyauhata surā vai mālyaṃ tato rājanyamasajata tasmājjāyāśca snuṣā ca

na pivet, pāpmanā na saṃsṛjyā iti | tadetadetatkṣastriyo brāhmaṇaṃ brūyānnainaṃ surā

pitṛṣvasrīyā duhitā veti pariṇayanābhyanujñānādvijñāyate |

[ prathamādhyāyasya
hananapratiṣedhe'pi bhavetpuṃstvaṃ vivakṣitam |
puṃstvavadekatvavivakṣāpi prasajyate ||
tathā
ahatvā kañcidevaikaṃ tataśca syātkṛtārthatā |
ekena ca surāpāne'varjite pūrvavadbhavet ||
āha yattāvadekatvavivakṣāgatamucyate |
pratyekavyaktisaṃbandhājjātervā tanna duṣyati ||
ekaikasyā eva hi brāhmaṇavyakteryathāprasaktavadhapānayoḥ pratiṣedhāt |
vivakṣyamāṇamapi tāvadanudyamānatvānna kriyāviśeṣaṇaṃ vā saṃbhavati kimuta yadā
jātigataikatvānuvāda evāyaṃ vijñāyate | āha | hananapratiṣedhe karmabhūtasya brāhmaṇa-
syoddiśyamānatvātsurāpāne ca kartṛtvenopādīyamānatvātsvargakāmodumbarīsaṃmānārtha

nuṣṭhānāditi | surāpānaliṅgavivakṣāvādinaṃ pratyatiprasaṅge codite sa
eva vadatītyāha - āheti | vaktavyamāha āha yattāvaditi | yattāvadubhaya-
traikatvaṃ vivakṣāgatamucyate | ahatvā kañcidevaikamiti | ekena surāpāne
iti ca | tatra brūmo vyaktiviśeṣaṇatvena vivakṣyamāṇamapyekatvaṃ na duṣyati |
yataḥ prativadhaṃ pratipānaṃ ca śāstramāvartate, tena na dvitīyāderbaṃdhapānaprasaṅgaḥ |
prativadhapānaṃ ca pratiśāstrapravṛtti ca prāptamevaikatvamato'nuvāda ekavacanaṃ
na tu kiśvidabhimataṃ vyavacchinatti | yena duṣyeta yata eva niṣphalamasya
vyaktiviśeṣaṇatvamanyato'pi viśeṣaṇārthaṃ siddherata eva jāterevedamucyate |
tenāpi na duṣyatīti | evamubhayatraikatvavivakṣāprasaṅgaparihāramuktvā hanana-

hanana pratiṣedha iti |

pratiṣedhavidhiparo hi vidhāyakaḥ śuddhavadhapratiṣedhāsaṃbhavād brāhmaṇajāti-

kā gatiḥ kalpasūtrāṇāmityasmin saṃśaye sati |
prayogaśāstratāṃ tāvat prāha sma tyaktasaṃśayam ||
vedatvaṃ kalpasūtrāṇāṃ na moktavyaṃ manāgapi |
yadi vā vedatulyatvaṃ svatantrāṇāṃ pratīyatām ||
vedenaivābhyanujñātā dharme teṣāṃ pramāṇatā |
vedatvameva vā siddhaṃ samvādādipramāṇakam ||
nanu ca pauruṣeyatvādeṣāmetadubhayamapyanupapannam |
naiteṣāṃ pauruṣeyatvaṃ bhaviṣyati hi vedavat |
māśakādisamākhyā hi proktatvātkāṭhakādivat ||
yathaiva kaṭhādiproktāḥ śākhā: kāṭhakādisamākhyayābhidhīyanta ityakṛtrimāḥ
sthāpitāstathaiva vedasamāmnātamaśakādisamākhyātagranthaniyatāpi pratyetavyā |
yathā sāmasūktānāmidamasyārṣamidamasyeti satyāmapi tannimittāyāṃ samākhyāyāṃ
na nityatvapratighātaḥ | evaṃ prakhyātarṣiprakalpasūtragranthānāmapi | tathā hi-
na tāvadanṛṣiḥ kaścitsmaryate kalpasūtrakṛt |
kartṛtvaṃ yadṛṣīṇāṃ tu tatsarvaṃ mantrakṛtsamam ||
yathā śaiśavaṃ bhavati śiśurvā aṅgiraso mantrakṛtāṃ mantrakṛdāsīdityatra
kāmaṃ na praviśed grāmamiti | yatheṣṭaṃ yena kenāpi vartmanā dharme
svāgamaprāmāṇyaṃ na pratipādayet | tāvat prāguktahetunirākṛto mīmāṃsakaiḥ |
samprati prakāśaṃ mahāpathenaiva āgamāpauruṣeyatvena tatprāmāṇyaṃ pratipādayi-
ṣyatīti - vedasamāmnāteti | ṣaḍaṅgameka iti vedatvena samāmnātasyeti |

kimuta yāni prasiddhāpabhraṣṭa deśabhāṣābhyo'pyapabhraṣṭatarāṇi bhikkhave ityeva-
mādīni dvitīyābahuvacanasthāne hyakārāntaṃ prākṛtaṃ padaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ na prathamā bahuvacane
saṃbodhane'pi | saṃskṛtaśabdasthāne ca kakāradvayasaṃyogo'nusvāralopaḥ, ṛvarṇā-
kārāpattimātrameva prākṛtāpabhraṃśeṣu dṛṣṭaṃ na ḍakārāpattirapi | so'yaṃ saṃskṛtā
dharma ityasya sarvakālaṃ svayameva pratiṣiddho'pi vināśaḥ kṛta ityasādhuśabda-
nibandhanatvādityantena hetunā vedatvākṛtakaśāstrāntaraśaṅkānivṛttiḥ | yāvāṃścā-
kṛtako vinaṣṭaḥ śabdarāśistasya vyākaraṇamevaikamupalakṣaṇaṃ tadupalakṣita-
rūpāṇi ca |
vede yathopalabhyante naivaṃ śākyādibhāṣite |
prayoganiyamābhāvādato'pyasya na śāstratā ||
asanniyamāditi ca vyākaraṇoktaniyamābhāvādityarthaḥ |
kṣaṇikatvanirākṛta nityasiddhānityatvātyantāvidyamānagrantha niyamābhyupagamā-

[ prathamādhyāyasya
tena yaḥ kaścidākhyātapratyayārthatattvaṃ jijñāsamānaḥ pṛcchati kaḥ punarayaṃ
bhāvo bhāvanā ceti taṃ pratyananyaśabdākhyeyatvāt kevalapratyayaprayogadarśanābhāvācca
sarvadhātvarthasāmānyavacanakarotipara liṅadyuccāraṇamātreṇaivottaraṃ dāsyate kuryā -
cchabda ityarthaḥ | sarvaścākhyāyamāno'rtha ākṛtirūpeṇa vākhyāyeta vyaktyākṛti -
gocarātikrāntiśca kilākhyāta śabdenopāyarahitatvādanākhyeyo'syārtha iti |
tadeva kilānenāpi granthenoktaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa | na tasyākṛtivacanatā nyāyyā
na vyaktivacanateti | vidhānaśabdo hi sūtre pūrvaṃ prakṛtastameva tacchabdena nirdiśya
vyaktyākṛtivacanatvādapanayati |
kuryādityucyamāno hi karomīti prapadyate |
na vidherbhāvanā yā vā sāmānyavyaktirūpatām ||
tadidamasaṃbaddhamevānupapattikaṃ ca śrutisāmānyamātreṇānyārthameva saccatuḥ-
śṛṅgādimantravadbhāṣyakārābhipretārthatyāgena svaprajñāvila sitaṃ
nyasminnevārthe yojitam |
prakāśayatā-
nanu vidhi: kārakavadanā 'khyeyatvābhidhānaṃ sahate bhāvanā tu sāmānya
viśeṣavatī bhāvārthādhikaraṇe darśitā sphuṭamākhyeyā bhavati | tatra kimiti
bhāvasyānākhyeyatāṃ vadati | tatrāha - vidhibhāvanayośceti | bhāva ityanā-
khyeyamiti | sāmānyaviśeṣātmanā svadharmeṇa bhāva iti bāhyaṃ vastu
śabdajñānādanyadanākhyeyam | sa hi manyate śabdotthāpite pravṛttinivṛttyanukūla-
jñāne eva vidhiniṣedho na vākye, mānāntarāsambhavāditi |
atha vānyadvastvantaraṃ bhāva ityākhyātaśabdotthāpitametacchabdajñānaṃ
pṛthak kṛtya śabdāntareṇānākhyeyamiti |
atha vātaḥ śabdajñānādanyato'nākhyeyamiti | avyayatvāt pañcabhya-
darśanamiti siddhāntamanākhyeyatāṃ kṛtvā ākhyātaśabdenaivottaraṃ pṛṣṭe
dātavyamityuktvā tāmevopapādayati - sarvaścākhyāyamāno'rtha iti | eva

yadi tu saṃvyavahāravākyedhvanupayujyamānamapi kāvyanāṭakalakṣaṇaprākṛta-

mātrālocanenāpi vā savibhaktikaprayājaprayogasiddheraśāstraṃ vyākaraṇam | 'yo
vā imāṃ padaśaḥ svaraśo'kṣaraśo vācaṃ vidadhāti sa ātvijīno bhavati' iti
samyagvedādhyāyyevamabhidhīyate | tathā 'catvāri vākparimitā padāni' iti |
yāni tāvadoṃkāramahāvyāhṛtyādicatuṣṭayabāhulyaprayojanānusaraṇena nairuktairapyakṣara-
varṇasāmyānnibrūyāditivat prapañcitāni na tatra vyākaraṇasya kaścidadhikāraḥ |
yattu nāmākhyātopasarganipātacatuṣṭayānugataṃ vaiyākaraṇamatamāśritaṃ tadapi catuṣṭaya-
sya lokasiddhatvādeva nātīva vyākaraṇāpekṣam | etadviṣayatve ca varṇyamāne
'turīyaṃ vāco manuṣyā vadanti' ityasaṃvaddhameva syāt | caturṇāmapi padajātānāṃ
manuṣyairucyamānatvāt | tasmādayamasya mantrasyārthaḥ paurvāparyasaṃgato varṇyate |
'catvāri vākparimitā padāni' iti | yairvāk padyate gamyate tāni catvāri pratyakṣā-
numānopamānārthāpattyākhyāni pramāṇānyabhidhīyante | tatra yāni pratīkavidhiprākṛta-
vaikṛtavākyasārūpyadṛṣṭānupapadyamānādiprabhavairanumānopamānārthāpattyākhyaistribhi
rgamyante, tāni tatsiddhatvādeva neṅgayanti noccārayanti | yastu bhāgastairaśakyaḥ
pratipādayituṃ taṃ turīyaṃ pratyakṣasamadhigamyamadhyetāro manuṣyā vadanti samāmanantī-
tyarthaḥ| ṣaṭpramāṇīmadhyācca pramāṇadvayaṃ vākpadatvādapoddhṛtam | abhāvastāvadabhāva-

[ 323
-
spaṣṭātmatattvajñānavidhānāpekṣitavākyāntaropāttadvividhābhyudayaniḥśreyasarūpaphala
saṃbandha: 'sa sarvāṃśca lokānāpnoti sarvāṃśca kāmānāpnoti tarati
śokamātmavittathā sa yadi pitṛlokakāmo bhavati saṃkalpādevāsya pitaraḥ
samuttiṣṭhanti tena pitṛlokena saṃpanno mahīyate' ityādinā yogajanyāṇimādyaṣṭa-
guṇaiśvaryaphalāni varṇitāni | tathā 'sa khalvenaṃ vartayan yāvadāyuṣaṃ
brahmalokamabhisaṃpadyate na sa punarāvartate' ityapunarāvṛttyātmakaparamātmaprāptya-
vasthāphalavacanam | aprakaraṇagatatvenānaikāntikakratu saṃvandhāsaṃbandhāccanāñjana-
khādirasra vavākyādiphalaśrutivadarthavādatvam |
na ca jñānavidhānena karmasambandhavāraṇam |
pratyāśramavarṇaniyatāni nityanaimittikakarmāṇyapi pūrvakṛtaduritakṣayārthama-
karaṇanimittānāgatapratyavāyaparihārārthaṃ ca karttavyāni | na ca teṣāṃ bhinnaprayo-
-
vidhināpekṣitāni trīṇi kāmavādādīni darśayati savīṃśca kāmāniti |
na caiṣāṃ vartamānāpadeśatvād yadāṅkte bhrātṛvyameva tad vṛṅkte yasya khādira:
suvo bhavati sa chandasāmeva rasenāvadyatītivadarthavādatvaṃ yatastayoḥ 'pārārthya-
jñānādevamavijñāte pārārthye rātrisatravadevaṃvidhamapi phalatvena vipariṇamya
gṛhyate | na cātra vijñānaṃ pārārthaṃ tatprāmāṇyasya prakaraṇasya vākyasya
vā vyabhicaritakratusaṃbandhadvārapravṛttasyābhāvādityāha
kāntikateti |
-
aprakaraṇagatāna-
nanu nityānāmagnihotrādīnāṃ janmamaraṇaparyantatvenāṅgīkaraṇāt kathaṃ
tadvirodhijñānaikaniṣṭhatvaṃ vidhātuṃ śakyamityāśaṅkyāha-na ca jñānavidhāneti |
naitaj jñānābhyāsavidhānaṃ sarvakarmavirodhena yasmād jñāturduritasya jñānapari-
pākavirodhino'bhāvāya jñānābhyāsenaiva nityāni karmāṇyapekṣitāni
nāpekṣitavirodhena kasyacid vidhirbhavati | yadaikaphalatvamekamārgastha puruṣa-
viṣayatvaṃ ca jñānakarmaṇoḥ syāttadā virodhād bādhaḥ sambhāvyeta tatparihārāya
vā vikalpāṅgāṅgibhāvāvāśrīyeyātām | karmāṇi tu viṣayendriyajasukha-
lolupasya bahiḥsādhanāyattaiśvaryakāmasya ca vidhīyante | jñānaṃ tu teṣu
viraktasya mumukṣoḥ saṅkalpasiddhi cecchata iti phalabhedo mārgabhedaśca | tathā
hi gṛhasthenāhavanīyādisādhanavatā karmāṇi kartavyāni parivrājakena
yamaniyamādisādhanavatā jñānamabhyasanīyam | ato bhinnādhikāriviṣaya
tvāt nāsti samavāyavirodha ityāha-na ca teṣāṃ bhinnaprayojanatvāditi |

uta samudāyaḥ, kimākṛtiviśiṣṭā vyaktiruta vyaktiviśiṣṭākṛtiriti | tathā

pravibhaktā hi ye dṛṣṭā dṛśyante saṃhatāḥ punaḥ |
piṇḍāsādhāraṇatvena tairvyaktirupalakṣyate ||
[ prathamādhyāyasya
na caikasyāṃ vyaktau ye samuditā dṛṣṭāsta eva vyaktyantare'pyanyūnānatiriktā
dṛśyante | ya evādṛṣṭapūrva stasminsamudāye saṃmprati dṛśyate sa evāsādhāraṇatāmāpāda-
yati | anantabhedāsvapi vyaktiṣu nātyantāpūrvaviśeṣaṇopalakṣaṇopādānaṃ kiñcidu-
tkarṣāpakarṣamātreṇa ca sarvatrāsādhāṇopalakṣaṇalābhāt parasāmānyāpekṣayā ca sarvāṇye-
vāvāntarasāmānyāni viśeṣavyapadeśaṃ prāpnuvanti | saṃghātāvasthāyāṃ ca vyakti-
viśeṣaṇātvātteṣu viśeṣaśabdaḥ | etacca vyaktaviśeṣāṇāmanyatvamupariṣṭād bhāṣya-
kāro'pi vakṣyatyeva, yo'rthaḥ sāmānyasya viśeṣāṇāṃ cāśrayaḥ sā vyaktiriti |
tatra kecid gotvādiṣu viśeṣatvamaṅgīkṛtya sāmānyavācinā śabdenāna-
bhiveyatvād yadeva mahāsāmānyaṃ sattāvastubhāvaśabdābhidheyaṃ tadeva śabdasvarūpavyava -
cchinnaṃ vā gosattākhyamākṛtyabhidhānapakṣe vācyamiti manyante | yathāhu:-
astyarthaḥ sarvaśabdānāmiti pratyāyyalakṣaṇam |
apūrvadevatāsvargaiḥ
samamāhurgavādiṣu || iti |
tattu kakṣyāvibhāgena sāmānyānāṃ nirūpaṇāt |
ayuktaṃ na gavādīnāṃ tatra vācakaśaktatā ||
pratiniyatārthaviṣayā hi śabdānāṃ vācakaśaktirarthāpattyā gamyate |

syāditi natrā tadabhāvaprasaṅgaṃ darśayati | yattu bhāvyakāro viparītārthena dravya-
śabdenaiva tadāśrayaguṇalakṣaṇayā natraścāsamarthasamāsamaṅgīkṛtya na dravyāśrayavacanaḥ

prayogāhaṃ cetyupekṣitavyam | tatrāpi caivamakṣarārthamātra saṃbhavayojanā | dravyāśrayasya
guṇasya yaḥ śabdo'dravyaśabdaḥ sa ākṛtenirguṇatvādekavākyasaṃbandhaṃ gotvaṃ śukla-

tvādyāśrayadoṣāprasaṅgaḥ | sā hi pratīyate niṣkṛṣṭarūpā, kriyāyoginī tu
tadekādhārasāmānyāntarāvagati-
viśiṣṭavyattayabhidhānapakṣamāśaṅkya dūṣayati - akṛtiviśiṣṭo'pi vyakti-
viśeṣa iti | kiñca yadyubhayātmakavastupratītiḥ syāttathāpyanyayavyatirekābhyāṃ

nānākāreṣu vastuṣviti | tattad viśeṣavyatireke'pi gośabdavācyatā śaktiranu-
gacchati sati sāmānyānugame tadvyatireke tu vyatiricyate | tena
gotvākāranibaddhaiva seti | nānākāreṣvapi cānvayavyatirekayoreva pramāṇyopa-
pādanaṃ yadi hi vastubhedānuvidhāyī śaktibhedo na bhavenna vyaktivyatirekeṇa
śaktyanugamādatacchaktitvāvadhāraṇaṃ syāditi | yadi śabdena na vyaktirna
bodhitā kathaṃ tadāśrayadravyatvasattādyavagatiḥ kathaṃ ca tadviśeṣeṇa śuklatvā-
dyākāṅkṣatyāha - tadekāghārasāmānyāntareti | nahi sākṣādanvaya eva
pratītyaṅgam kintu anvayamātram | tena gotvarūpasyārthasya ekārthasamavāyinyā

dvitīyādāviyaṃ yasmād vistareṇa kariṣyate ||
kaḥ punarbhāva ityādī vivekenārthanirṇayaḥ |
ākhyātasya kṛtastena taccintā nopapadyate ||
pratyayārthasya bhāvasya vācyatā pratipāditā |
prakṛtyarthavivekārthaṃ vicāraḥ kriyate'dhunā ||
pratyayasyāpi vā tatra bhāvārthatve nirūpite |
sāmānyaṃ vā viśeṣo vā kiṃ vācyamiti cintyate ||
yā coktā bhāṣyakāreṇa yāgāderabhiveyatā |
siddhā seha vivekārthaṃ tatra bhāvasya bhāṣitā ||
yajyādyarthe'pi vā vācye kiṃ sāmānyaviśeṣayoḥ |
abhidheyamiti prāptā cintā kartuṃ kriyāpade ||
apūrvaṃ bhāvaśabdebhyo dravyādibhyaḥ kimiṣyate |
eṣā tatra ca cintoktā śeṣā prāsaṅgikī kathā ||
nāmākhyātapade tena kasyārthasyābhidhāyake |
kimākṛteruta vyakteriti cintā pravarttate ||
prayogasya pratīteśca tulyatvācchabdalakṣaṇe |
vyaktipakṣābhidhānācca saṃśayaḥ pratibhāti naḥ ||
kathaṃ lakṣaṇasambandhāścintāyāḥ prakṛtena vā |
smṛtimūlavicāreṇa vaktavyaṃ tvidamāditaḥ ||
iyaṃ prāsaṅgikī cintā sādhuśabde nirūpite |
udāhṛtasya tasyaiva kriyate'rthaviniścayaḥ ||
vaktavyaḥ
pūrvamarthaścecchabdarūpanirūpaṇāt |
na hi vācyamavijñāya sādhutvamavadhāryate ||
aniścite'pi vācyatve vyaktyākṛtyorvivekataḥ |

pattistatraivārthāpattyā vyaktivacanatvaṃ yuktam, anyatra tu pūrvapratīterjātivacanatvameva
nyāyyamiti cet | na | uktamārgeṇānekaśaktikalpanādidoṣaprasaṅgāt |
anavasthitaśabdārtha saṃbandhāpatteḥ saṃśayāpatteśca | vyaktivādinaḥ kathaṃ nirnimittatā
vyaktyantare śabdasya pravṛttiriti cedākṛticihnanimittatvād vyaktyantare
prayogasyāvirodhaḥ | bhāṣyakārastu kathaṃ sāmānyāvagatirityupanyasyākṛtiścihnabhūtā
bhaviṣyatītyuttaraṃ dadāti tadasaṃvaddham | kathaṃ sāmānyapratyayaḥ kāraṇe pṛṣṭe
vyaktyantaraprayoganimittaṃ kathyate | tenādhyāhāreṇaitad vyākhyeyam | kathaṃ sāmānyā-
vagatiriti cet | vyaktisaṃbandhāt | kathaṃ vyaktyantare prayogaḥ, cihnatvādākṛte-
rityevam | yadvā kathaṃ sāmānyāvagatiriti cedityupanyaste pūrvapakṣavādī sāmānyā-
vagatimapahnotumaśaknuvannākṛtiviśiṣṭavyaktyabhidhānapakṣaṃ parigṛhṇātyākṛti-
ścihnabhūtā viśeṣaṇabhūtābhidheyā bhaviṣyati | ataśca tatpratītiracodyā | tena
kevalā viśiṣṭā vā vyaktiḥ śabdārthaḥ | tathā ca 'ṣaḍ deyā' ityevamādipu
ṣaḍādikā saṃkhyopapatsyate | anyaṃ tadvarṇamiti ca vyaktyantare'nyaśabdopapatti-
staddharmatā ca bhaviṣyati | anyathā vyaktyantarānayane'pi jāterananyatvādaguṇa-
tvāccobhayamanupapannaṃ, tasmād vyaktiḥ śabdārtha iti prāpte'bhidhīyate | naitat |
vyaktiḥ śabdavācyeti kiṃ tvākṛtiḥ padārtha iti vijñāyate | 'śyenacitaṃ cinvīta'
iti śravaṇāt | atra hi śyenavyakti cayanena kuryādākṛti veti vāvayārthau syātām |
yāvadiṣṭakābhiḥ śyenavyakteḥ kartumaśakyatvāt snāyvādibhirapyanivṛ tteriṣṭakā-
bādhācchya enavyakteḥ prayojana kalpanāccinoteramukhyārthatvāt | 'karmaṇyagnyā-
khyāyām [pā0 sū0 3|62|] iti smṛtiparityāgaprasaṅgācca | cayanena śyena-
vyakti kuryādityevaṃ tāvannopapadyate | tathākṛterapīṣṭakābhiḥ kartumaśakyatvāddaiva-
nirmitatvāt prayojanakalpanāccinoterayathārthatvaprasaṅgātsmṛtibādhāccākṛti kuryā -
dityapi nopapadyate | pariśeṣācchyanamiva citamagnisthalaṃ cayanena nirvarttayediti

tena ca vyavahārābhāvādabhidhānānarthakyam | samudāyivināśe ca tasyāpyanitya-

iti | tadetanna yujyate | yuktaṃ devadattagṛhasya puruṣasya ca kevalasyābhidhānaṃ
viśiṣṭapratītāvapi kākanilayanadaṇḍayoḥ pratyakṣadaṇḍaśabdāvagatayoviśeṣaṇa-
tvopapatteḥ, na tu gotvasyāsannihitasye hopalakṣaṇatvaṃ yujyate | apratotaviśeṣaṇe
viśeṣyāpratīterna ca daṇḍiśabdavattadavayavena gotvābhidhānaṃ pratīmo, yena dvitīyā-
vayavena vyakterevābhidhānaṃ syāt | ataśca yadi tāvadgotvamabhidhīyate tata-
stadevoktena nyāyena vācyaṃ bhavet | apratītasya viśeṣaṇatvāsaṃbhavāt | uktavadati -
prasaṅgaḥ śabdaprayogasya prāpnoti | na ca yathā daṇḍiśabda: kevale daṇḍe na prayujyata
iti viśiṣṭavacano'vadhāryate tadvadākṛtau prayogābhāvād viśiṣṭavacano'vadhārayituṃ
śakyate| śyenacityādāvākṛtī prayogadarśanādanvayavyatirekābhyāṃ ca jātireva
vācyetyavadhāryate, śyenacidvākya kevalāyāmākṛtau prayuktatvāt | kvacidapi
cākṛtyā vinā vyaktimātre prayogādarśanādeva ye'pi sambandha samudāyādayaḥ pūrvapakṣā-
ste'pyuktena nyāyena nirākṛtāḥ | tenākṛtireva śabdārtha iti ||33||
pratyāyyata
ye ‘tvālambhanādicodanābhāvādayo 'na kriyā syāt' [mī0 sū0 1|3|34|]
ityetat sūtrānubhāṣitāḥ pūrvapakṣoktāḥ hetavasteṣāṃ sarveṣāmanena sūtreṇa parihāro'bhi-
dhīyate - tadarthatvātprayogasyāvibhāgāḥ [mī0sū0 1|3|35] iti | tadarthatvāt -
apūrvārthatvādālabhbhanaprokṣaṇādyaṅgānāmapūrvasādhane prayogeṇa bhavitavyaṃ dravyaṃ cāpūrva-
sādhanaṃ yāgena svasiddhyarthamākṣiptatvānnākṛtirasamarthatvāttadabhidhānaṃ tu dravyaniyamārthaṃ
lakṣaṇamiti vaktavyam | yataḥ svayameva daṇḍaviśiṣṭa: |
ityabhidhānena viśeṣaṇatvamasyāṅgīkṛtamiti taddarśayituṃ bhāṣyākṣarāṇyāha
viśiṣṭaḥ pratyāyyata iti | tadupalakṣaṇasyāpīti | viśeṣaṇasāmānya-
mupalakṣaṇe'pyastīti | bhavatu viśeṣaṇopalakṣaṇayoratyantabhedastathāpya-
doṣa ityāha-yadvā kenacitsāmānyeneti [ ? ] | na ca dṛṣṭārthopapattirityāha-
'sādhanatvāpratīteriti | sādhanasya kriyānirvarttakatvenārādupakāritā | tena

na sāmānyasyānarthakatvam | parasparasaṃnidhānena hi nāmāpi yāgaviṣayaṃ yāgos -
pyetadavacchinno na sāmānyamātraṃ prakṛto veti labhyate | tasmādubhayorarthavatā
viśiṣṭabhāvanāvidhānāccobhayorapi vidhānamiti nānuvādatvam | evaṃ ca sati
na nāmatvaṃ vidhitvamātrapratiyogīti, ye pūrvapakṣe vidhyantarbhāvaṃ siddhānte tu vidhyartha-
vādamantrātirekaṃ nāmnāṃ varṇayanti teṣāmayuktameva | 1 aśeṣavyavahārāṅgabhāvācca
nāpravṛttiviśeṣakaratā | nahya tvigvaraṇādiṣvanenāhaṃ yakṣya iti laghurākhyānopāyaḥ
syāt| darśapūrṇamāsāvārapsyamānaḥ 'same darśapūrṇamāsābhyām, iti guṇopabandho

dheyānyevaṃjātīyakānīti siddham ||2||

yathaiva 'agnimupanidhāya stuvate' ityatrāgniḥ sattāmātreṇopakarotyevamihāpīti
nirdoṣaṃ, tasmād guṇavidhānamiti | atrābhidhīyate 'pradhānato'bhisaṃbandha' iti |
anekārthaṃvidhānaṃ hi pradhānakarmavidhāne satyupapadyate | atra punaḥ karmānuvādena
guṇo vidhīyate | na ca guṇānāṃ parasparaṃ saṃbandho vidyate | tatra kaguṇavidhāne
guṇāntarānākṣepādarthāpattyabhāve śrautānekavidhivyāpārakalpanāyāṃ punaḥ puna-
ruccāraṇaṃ pratyayasya kartavyamiti vākyabhedaḥ syāt | āha ca -
arthādenakamapyarthaṃ vidhāpayati bhāvanā |
viśeṣaṇavidhistvanyanna gṛhṇāti viśeṣaṇam ||
ekapadopātte'pi cānekavidhiśaktikalpanāgauravamastyeva | utpattivākya-
śiṣṭe guṇāntarāvirodhāccāśaktiḥ | tathā hi-
utpattivākyavijñātaṃ karma sarvaṃ hyanudyate |
na cāśritaguṇe śakyaṃ tadvirodhi guṇāntaram ||
yatrānūdyamāna evāvaśyaṃ guṇo'pyanuvaditavyastatra nairapekṣyadarśanādākāṅkṣā-
dhīna saṃbandhaguṇāntaravidhānānupapattiḥ | na ca niṣkṛṣya kriyāmātraṃ guṇavidhiḥ śakyaḥ
kartum | kutaḥ—
prathamaṃ hi svavākyasthairguṇaiḥ saṃbadhyate kriyā |
vākyāntaragataiḥ paścānna śuddhā sā hyavāpyate ||
yāni parasparanirapekṣāṇi vrīhiyavādivākyavat pravartante teṣāṃ tulyabala-
vattvād bhavati vikalpaḥ | iha cotpattivākyamanyanirapekṣaṃ vidadhyānnotpannavākyam,
anutpanne'nuvādānupapatteḥ | paśukāmasaṃbandhācca padāntaragatārthagrahaṇanimitto'pi
vākyabhedo bhidyate | yadyapi ca phalaṃ na vidhīyate tathāpi tatra karmavidherastyeva
gauravam | api ca karmaṇa: phalaguṇau prati yugapadvidhyanuvādadoṣo'paraḥ syāt |
na ca paśukāmapadamarjanāṅgatvāt prāptamanudyate | katham -

tatsādhanatadaṅgeṣu pravṛttiḥ prārthanādṛte ||

tasya viśiṣṭaviṣayatve śuddhenāpi kriyāśabdena viśiṣṭamevopasthāpyate'to
viśeṣaṇāntarākāṅkṣānudaya iti |

[ prathamādhyāyasya
na hi sādhanabhūtaṃ paśuṃ kaścitkāmayate | vinaiva tu kāmena prārthitānyathā-
nupapattyā tamupādatte | gauṇī vā tasya kāmyatā bhavet | ataḥ svātantryeṇa
yanmukhyayā vṛttyā kāmyate, tadeva kāmaśabdopasambandhātphalaṃ pratīmaḥ | vistareṇa
caṃtad 'asādhakaṃ tattādarthyāt' [mī0 sū0 6 |1|2| ] ityatra vakṣyāmaḥ | phalaṃ cāgnī-
ṣomoyasya kratvaṅgatvānnaitadanyād vāstītyanuvādāsambhavaḥ | prakaraṇañca bādhyate
prājāpatyasya yāgasya | nāmadheyatve tu karmaphalasambandhamātrakaraṇānna kaścid doṣaḥ |
'pañcadaśānyājyāni' iti dravyasaṃkhyayorubhayoḥ stotreṣvaprāptatvānnānyatarānuvāde
netaravidhānasambhavaḥ| karmāntarasthañca na pratīyate | tadanuvādena ca vidhāvakarmā-
ṅgatvaprasaṅgaḥ prakaraṇabādhaśca | na ca viśiṣṭadravyavidhirupapadyata iti vakṣyate-
tatraikatvamayajñāṅgabhūtam iti | na cākriyāviśeṣaṇaṃ vidhīyate, vidhāyakasya

guṇamākṣipet| na caiṣāṃ stutiprayogavacanena grahaṇam, akriyātmakatvāt |

evaṃ siddheṣu nāmaveyeṣu nāmaveyakāryeṣu cedānīmapavādaḥ prastūyate | kasmāt -
punarihaiva davihomapadaṃ na vicāryate, vicāritametadapi tatprakhyamityanena | vidhyu

nopanyastam | yā tu tatra guṇavidhyāśaṅkā sā'dhikaraṇamadhye paricodanāmātraṃ
sarvamevaitatprāsaṅgikam | aṣṭame ca pūrvavadapūrvacintādhikārāt | kena punarvihitānāṃ
karmaṇāmāgneyādīnāṃ nāmadheyatvamāśaṅkayate | taducyate | aṣṭākapālo bhavatītyā-
-
vidhyuddeśyagatānāṃ ceti | vidhitvena prāmāṇyamabhidhāya tadviśeṣa-
vicāro yataḥ | yattu vicāritamevetyuktam tattulyanyāyatayā | yadi nāmadheyatva-
prasiddhaye tatra davihomapadamudāhariṣyate tadā tadvadatrodāharaṇīyatā codyeta |
siddhaṃ tviha nāmadheyatvamaṅgīkṛtya kasya nāmeti viśeṣacintā tatra
kariṣyata ityata āha - siddhe vā karmanāmadheyatva iti | nanu nāmadheyatva 2
sāmānyamapi tatra guṇavidhitvāśaṅkāṃ nirākṛtya kariṣyate | ata āha
yā tviti | kiśva tulyanyāyatayā sādhitamapi nāmadheyatvaṃ yadi tatra
prapañcyate tathāpi paunaruktyam | yato davihomānāṃ pūrvavadapūrvatāṃ vicārayituṃ
davihomapadena sarveṣāmupādānaṃ kurvan nāmadheyatvaṃ prasaṅgād vakṣyati | kriyate
ca sāmānyataḥ siddhasyaḥ prasaṅgādudāharaṇāntare yojanamityāha -- sarvameveti |

vidhikalpanamagatikā gatiḥ | smṛtestu vidhikalpanameva gatiḥ | evaṃ
davihomapada vācyatvenāvadhāriteṣu homeṣu cintitaṃ kimapūrvāste uta
kutaściddharmagrāhiṇa iti | tatrāvyaktacodanātvāvāntarasāmānyena jyoti-
ṣṭomavidhyantaprāpti mahāsāmānyasyaiva yāgatvasya davihome'pyabhāvā-
nnirākṛtā | tato nāriṣṭān juhotīti coditānāmiṣṭyaṅgānāṃ nāriṣṭa-
homānāṃ vidhyantaprāptiḥ | keṣuciddavihomeṣu tadīyavidhyantābhāvānuvāda-
darśanānnirākṛtā apratiṣṭhā vai tryambakā ityāhuḥ | nātreduddhamā bahiḥ
sannahyate na sāmidhenyo'nūcyante iti hi siddhavannāriṣṭhahomavidhyantabhāva-
stryambakābhidheyeṣu davihomeṣūcyate | kimiti saha patnyā juhotīti
coditānāṃ patnīsaṃyājānāmiddhamādyaṅgarahitānāṃ vidhyanto na syādi
tyāśaṅkaya niṣphalatvena dūṣitam | dravyasaṃskāramātraprāptireva tatra syāt |
na ca patnīsaṃyājadravyasyājyasya saṃskārād davihomadravyeṣu oṣadhīṣū -
pakurvantyauṣadhadravyakayostu piṣṭa lepaphalīkaraṇa homayovidhyantāsambandhe

[ prathamādhyāyasya
dhāritāṅgatvamantrānurodhenaivaṃ vijñāyate yānete vadanti te nūnamagnyādayaḥ kriyāṅga-
miti | te ca mantreṣu prādhānyābhidhānāddevatātvena prajñātā iti tadavailakṣaṇyāt

dāne gṛhīte codanāntareṇa ca tatsaṃskārārthopastaraṇābhighāraṇagrahaṇāccaturavatte

prāpte'bhidhīyate—
na cedanyena śiṣṭāḥ syuryāgāḥ śabdena kenacit |
te guṇāścopadiśyeran vidhinā hyavibhāgataḥ |
na tāvad dravyadevatāsaṃskārasaṃyogena yāgavidhirupapadyate | katham ?

dhyeran | agnyādyabhidhāyināṃ na kaścittaiḥ saṃbandho vidyate |
na ca yāgo haviṣṭvaṃ vā puroḍāśe'vadhāritam |
yena syād devatākāṅkṣā tanmantrāṇāṃ ca saṃgatiḥ ||

satyatideśavaiphalyamato'pūrvā davihomāḥ prasiddhāstadavailakṣaṇyāditi | yanmantre'-

karmaṇastatrāpi devatā bhavantīti | pratiniyatakartṛtvāditi | bhavanamasiddha-
kartṛkameva na siddhakartṛkam | yathā puroḍāśasamaveto vyāpārastatkartṛ ka
eva nānyakartṛ kastat prayuktayā tvanyaḥ kartā syāditi || ||

nānāvidheyaprayojanavaśenārthaikatvābhāvāt | na ca nānāvidhānāṃ vibhajyamānānāṃ
sākāṅkṣatvaṃ vidyate | yadi caikaṃ vākyaṃ bhavettataḥ sarvaviśiṣṭakabhāvanā-
vidhānānna vikalpaḥ syāt | aṣṭatvādīnāṃ ca guṇatvānna pratyekaṃ vākyaparisamāptyā
pṛthaganuṣṭānopapattiḥ | na ca saṃkhyānāṃ samuccayaḥ saṃbhavati | saṃkhyāntaropajananena
sarvābhāvaprasaṅgāt | saṃkhyāntarānupajananasamuccayastu dvādaśatve'ṣṭatvādyantarbhāvāt |
tathābhūtānāṃ ca dvādaśatvavidhinaiva prāpteranuvādatvāpattiḥ | tenaitaibhinnairvākyaiḥ
svatantrā vidhīyanta ityavaśyaṃ bhavatābhyupagantavyam | tataśca dvādaśatvamutpatti-
vākyaśiṣṭamitarāṇi tu vākyāntaraiḥ prakaraṇāpekṣairdurbalāni santi na saṃbadhyante |

tatravattikam
puruṣārthaṃ samāsatteḥ kāmyaṃ nityasya bādhakam |
viśeṣataśca sāmānye pūrvaṃprāpte pravartanāt ||
[ prathamādhyāyasya
puruṣārthasiddhyartho hi sarva: prayāsa iti tatpratyāsannaṃ vastu balīyo
bhavati | dūre ca dvādaśatvaṃ puruṣārthasya tatsādhanayāgadravya saṃskārārthakapālapari-
cchedāt | aṣṭatvādayastu tadāśritāḥ santaḥ sākṣād brahmavarcasādi sādhayantīti
pratyāsatterbalīyāṃso bhavanti | tasmādarthavanta iti | nanu ca -
brahmavarcasayogādi phalatvena yadīṣyate |
stutyabhāvādvidhiḥ kena vartamānāpadeśitām ||
tatra kecidāhuḥ, audumbarādhikaraṇapūrvapakṣanyāyenātra pratyavasthānamiti |
tanna yuktam | tatraiva dattottaratvāt | ato'yamabhiprāyaḥ | 'pūta eva sa tejasvī '
ityādinā phalamucyate | 'gāyatryaivaitam' ityādistuteḥ prātyātmiko vidhiḥ |
'yadaṣṭākapāla' ityādibhiḥ sārthavādakaiḥ pratyekaṃ vihitānāṃ yathāsaṃkhyena pūta eve-
tyādibhiḥ phalasaṃbandhaḥ kriyate || 21||
vākyāni yāvadarthaṃ syurguṇebhyaḥ phalakalpane |
ekopakramasaṃhārādekaṃ cedaṃ pratīyate ||
putre jāte dvādaśakapālamiti yadevopakrāntaṃ tadevāṣṭatvādīnāmante 'yad
dvādaśakapālo bhavati' iti 'yasmin jāta etām' iti copasaṃhriyamāṇaṃ
paśyāmaḥ | tāvati ca nāṣṭatvādayaḥ pṛthg bhavituṃ labhante tadekavākyatā ca na
stutyā vinopapadyate | arthāntaraparatāyāmānarthakyāt | yadi hi kartṛ saṃvandhīnye-
tāni bhaveyustatastasya kāraṇāni prayojakāni syuḥ | vaiśvānarasaṃbandhīni tvetāni |
viśeṣataśca sāmānya iti | anena sāmānyaviśeṣalakṣaṇaṃ balābala -
muktam | pūrvaprāpte pravartanādityanena paurvāparyalakṣaṇamiti | pūta eva sa tejasvī-
tyādineti | yadaṣṭākapālo bhavati gāyatryaivaitaṃ brahmavarcasena punāti ityevaṃ
yāvadddvādaśakapālaṃ tejo'nnādyendriyaḥ paśavaḥ kīrtitāstaiḥ stutirante ca pūta
eva tejasvyānnāda indriyāvī paśumān bhavatīti yathāsaṃkhyena phala vidhaya
iti ||21||

tatra sāmarthyasiddhena vidhyaṃśenaiva nirṇayaḥ ||
devatāprasādābhayaprārthanādau saṃhatahastasaṃyogamātramañjaliḥ, 'nāñjalinā
vāri pibet' ityādau 'vyākośaḥ | saknuhomacodanāyāṃ śabdāt sandehe satyasati
cākrośasya vākyaśeṣe'nyathānupapatterevākkrośāśrayaṇam | nanvanenaiva nyāyena yathā
śaknuyāditi vākyaśeṣādandhādīnāmājyāvekṣaṇādirahitaḥ karmādhikāraḥ syāt,
atha vā vināpi cārthavādena vidhyuddeśa ityavirodhādityasya vyākhyānam |
na vidherarthavādābhāve kaścid virodho yena tadṛśaḥ syāditi | yadyapi
tāvaditi kriyayā sambandhādupātteṣvapyanekeṣu sādhaneṣu ekasyaiva ghṛtasya
vidhānāditareṣāmupādānaṃ yadyapiśabdena sūcitamupāttamaśakyamarthākṣepasya
śabdena ghṛtena bādhitatvāditi | yadvakṣyati kriyāmātraṃ tvihetyādinā iti |
kālpanikī vākya ṃ bhinattīti ghṛtaprarocanāmupādāya samāpte añjanavidhau
kālpanikyāṃ tailādiprarocanāyāṃ vākyamārabhyamiti evaṃ kriyayārthā-
kṣepāt tailādibodhane'pi teṣāmupādāna'mayuktamityuktaṃ samprati śabda
saṃsparśo'pyevameveti vadati - yadyapi ca sāmānyaśabdaḥ syāditi | taccaitad vāsaḥ
paridhatta ityādiṣūpayujyata ityucyate | tatra ṣaṣṭāntyeneti [?] | yadi
bhinnavākyagatasyāviśeṣaśabdasyātraiva niyāmakatvamuktamucyate tasyāviṣaya-
tvamekavākyagatasyātrokte bhinnavākyagatasya tatrocyata ityupapannam |24|
atha yatreti saṃdigdha iti | iti karaṇena pūrvādhikaraṇasandeha
parāmarśaḥ viśeṣastu śabdasāmarthyayovisaṃvādādatrāsau vidhigatenāpya śabdo'rthe
pravṛtterasaṃbhavāt prarocanāvagatāvapyapekṣāvidhiḥ sā caikasmin dṛśyate na hi
tadanurūpaḥ śabdaḥ tasya sāmānyena pravṛtteḥ | tatra śabdapramāṇake'rthe sāmarthya-
1. ityādāvākrośaḥ iti kāśīmudritapustakapāṭhaḥ |
1. 'mayuktamiti nāsti ā0 mā0 |
2. gatenāpyaśakye pravṛttera ā0 mā0 |

tantravārtikam
434 ]
[ prathamādhyāyasya
na | asaṃdehāt, saṃdigdha nirṇayārthāttvevaṃ kalpanā bhavati | na cādhikāre sandehaḥ |
samastadharmopetaṃ yathā śaknuyāttathā kuryāditi vākyaśeṣāt | santi ca tādṛśasya
kartāraścakṣuṣmadādaya' iti na prayogacodanānyathānupapattiḥ | yatra tu yāvajjīva-
codanādāvevaṃ bhaviṣyati tatra śaktikṛtāṅgayāge'pi satyavaiguṇyaṃ pradhānasya
coditamityāśrayiṣyāmaha eva | tasmād yāvadeva labdhātmako vidhistāvadyat
kalpate tadvaidikaṃ, siddhe tu tatkalpanaṃ pauruṣeyatvādapramāṇaṃ sāmarthyādhīnatvād
vākyaśeṣasiddheḥ | āha ca---
mātrādapratīteḥ sāmarthyarahite ca śabdapravṛtteḥ asaṃbhavādubhayapakṣānavadhāraṇe
tṛtīyapakṣāsaṃbhavācca sandehaḥ - nāsandehāditi | yatra hi śabdaḥ samānya-
vṛttirekatrāpi svārthaṃ labhamāno gṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvāt sarvatra prayujyate
tatra viśeṣagrahaṇe sāmarthyamasti nāstīti jāyate sandehaḥ | yatra tu
śabdena viśeṣa evopātte tatrāsāvasandigdha evāsau upāttaśca śabdena
sāṅgo'dhikārastatrānupapattisāmānyapravṛttisvargakāmādyadhikāripadasāmarthyato
viśeṣaviṣayatvena parihīyate nāṅgaprahāṇena ! yatra tu sāmarthyata
evādhikāritvena śabdaviṣayīkṛtasya kādācitkatvenāsāmarthyena nimitta-
vaśādadhikāro na vyāvatiṣyate | tatrāśaktyāṅgatyāgenaivādhikāraṃ pratyeṣyata
ityāha-yatra tu yāvajjīveti | sarvaśaktyadhikaraṇa etad vakṣyate | yāvad
jīvannagniṃ juhuyāditi | tatrāṅgānāṃ pratyekaṃ svaśabdenābhihitānāṃ tyāge
śabdavirodhānnāṅgatyāgaḥ | jīvanaśrutistu sāṅgaśaktikālīnajīvane
kṛtārthaiveti | yadā jīvanaṃ śaknuyāt tadā sāṅgaṃ kuryādityadhikārāvagamāt
sarvāṅgopasaṃhāreṇa prayoga iti pūrvapakṣa: |
siddhāntastu yadi satyapi jīvane karma kartavyaṃ na syāt tato jīvanasya
nimittatvameva na syāt | ato yāvajjīvaṃ kartavyatāvagamādadhikāraśrutito
durbalānāmaṅgaśrutīnāmeva śaktito vyavastheti | tasmād yāvadevālabdhātmako
vidhiriti | yāvadaparaṃ śāstraprameyaṃ śrūyate na śabdenāpekṣitaṃ bodhitaṃ cāsti
tāvadartha sāmarthya vyāpāraḥ | parisamāpte tu viśeṣanirṇaye śāstre yatkalpyate
tadaśabdamūlatvādapramāṇam |
1. cakṣuṣpadādayaḥ kāśī mudritapustakapāṭhaḥ |
1. ya eva hi śabdaḥ ā0 mā0 |
2. parihi yate ā0 mā0 |

caturthaḥ pādā ]
ajitopetam
yathaiva pāṭhaḥ pratipattyupāyastathaiva sāmarthyamapi śrutīnām |
tenaiva caitā na samāmananti sahasrabhāgaṃ tu samāmaneyuḥ ||
[ 435
iti kṛtabahumāgaṃ mantravidhyarthavādaiḥ smṛtiparikhamudrāṃ khedaśālopagūḍhām |
anabhibhavasamarthaṃ dharmadurgaṃ praviśya pravibhajati samarthāmarthacintāmidānīm ||25|
iti śrībhaṭṭakumārilaviracite mīmāṃsābhāṣyavyākhyāne
tantravārtike
prathamasyādhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ |
tathā hi sāmarthyasyāpramāṇatvāt tataḥ sarvaṃ vākyaśeṣaḥ kalpanīyaḥ | sāmānya-
śāstrasya 'viśeṣākāṅkṣasya tacchandaśeṣaḥ sambhavati na viśeṣasamāptasya
viśeṣāntaraśabdasya viruddhatvādityāha - sāmarthyādhīnatvāditi | mataṃ
caitad vṛddhānāṃ yatsāmarthyāt vākyaśeṣaḥ kalpyate iti darśayati
āha ceti | prameyajñānaprayuktaṃ pramāṇanirūpaṇamāgatam | yāvati ca pramāṇa-
nirūpaṇe kṛte prameyaṃ nirūpayituṃ śakyate tāvattena prayujyate | kṛtaṃ ca tāvatā
tato nirūpite pramāṇe tadupāyanirūpaṇamidānīṃ sūtrakāraḥ karotīti vadan
pramāṇalakṣaṇāvasānaṃ prameyasaṅgati ca dvayamāha - iti kṛtabahumārgamiti |
laukikasya hiraṇyāderarthasya cintānekaprakāra mārgopetaṃ parikhāprākāra-
lakṣitamudāratvādasaṃkaṭamapahārapadairarthalipsubhiranabhibhavasamayaṃ durgaṃ praviśya
kriyate vedārthasyāpyartha śabdavācyatā sāmānyena cintā laukika-
cintayā rūpayatītyuktaprakāraparāmarśo mantravidhyarthavādanāṃ prasthānabhedāddhamaṃ
durgāptihetutvādaneko mārgaḥ | smṛtestadrakṣā hetutvāt prākārākhyavedavyavadhinā
strotvena ca parikhāsāmyaṃ vedasya rakṣāhetutvena durgavyavadhinā ca śālaparyāya
prākārasāmyaṃ ebhirupetatvāddhetukairanabhibhavasamarthamudāraṃ dharmākhyaṃ vāhana*tvā-
